Projection robust:
```
def project_feasible(h,target=None):
 n=len(h); target=n/2
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 for _ in range(1000):
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
  if diff>0:
    free=np.where(h<1-1e-14)[0]
    if len==0: break
    room=1-h
    # distribute proportional to room? To minimize L2 use water filling: find lambda? but scaling by room simple.
    h[free]+=diff*room[free]/room[free].sum()
  else:
    free=np.where(h>1e-14)[0]; h[free]+=diff * h[free]/h[free].sum()
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 # fix residual on one coordinate
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)>1e-12:
   if diff>0: idx=np.argmax(1-h); h[idx]=min(1,h[idx]+diff)
   else: idx=np.argmin(h); h[idx]=max(0,h[idx]+diff)
 if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-10:
   # scale free? 
 return h
```

Gradient function with weights over full:
```
def smooth_weights(c, alpha):
  # c vector same length, maybe scaled by dx? max scale irrelevant but weights normalized.
  cmax = c.max()
  s = alpha*(c - cmax)
  # stable: max_s = s.max() (zero)
  w = np.exp(s - s.max())
  w /= w.sum()
  return w
```
If alpha huge and c close, exp(-|diff|*alpha) underflow to zeros; if one active weight 1. That's okay. For L-BFGS objective, need smooth value and gradient. Use same weights.

```
def gradient_smooth_max(h, n, w, dx):
    # w length 2n-1
    c0w = w[n-1]
    grad = (1.0 - 2.0*h)*c0w
    if n > 1:
        L = w[:n-1][::-1]  # left of center in order p=n-1..1; we need L[p]=w[n-1-p]
        R = w[n:]          # R[p-1]=w[n+p-1]=w[n-1+p]
        # build arrays length n:
        Lp = np.zeros(n); Lp[1:] = L[::-1]? Wait w[:n-1] order index0..n-2 correspond p=n-1..1. We need W_plus[p]=w[n-1-p]. For p=1 -> w[n-2] (last of left). So Lp[1:] = w[n-2::-1]? In numpy: left = w[:n-1]; pvals = np.arange(n-1,0,-1) maybe.
        Lp = np.empty(n); Lp[0]=0; Lp[1:] = w[n-2:-1?][::-1]? Let's test n=5 full len9 center4. left indices 0,1,2,3 for p=4,3,2,1. W_plus[1]=w[3], [2]=w[2], [3]=w[1], [4]=w[0]. So Lp[1:] = w[3:0:-1] (indices 3,2,1,0). `Lp[1:] = w[n-2::-1]` for n=5: w[3::-1] gives 3,2,1,0 good.
        Rp = np.empty(n); Rp[0]=0; Rp[1:] = w[n:] (w[5],w[6],w[7],w[8] for p1..4)
        S = Lp + Rp
        # linear
        # prefix_R[i]=sum_{p=1}^i Rp[p]
        pref_R = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(Rp[1:]))) # length n
        # suffix_L[i]=sum_{p=i+1}^{n-1} Lp[p]
        suffix_L = np.zeros(n)
        # cumulative from right: total_L = Lp[1:].sum(); for i: suffix_L[i]=total_L - sum_{p=1}^i Lp[p]
        cum_L = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(Lp[1:])))
        total_L = cum_L[-1]
        suffix_L = total_L - cum_L  # for i=0 total; i=n-1 total-cum_L[n-1]=0
        lin = suffix_L + pref_R
        # quadratic q:
        # f[i]=sum_{p=1}^{n-1-i} S[p] h[i+p]
        # b[i]=sum_{p=1}^{i} S[p] h[i-p]
        # Use FFT length N >= 2n-1.
        N = 1
        while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
        Fh = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
        Fs = np.fft.rfft(S, N)
        Fs_rev = np.fft.rfft(S[::-1], N)
        convS = np.fft.irfft(Fh * Fs, N)  # h * S
        b = convS[:n]
        convSr = np.fft.irfft(Fh * Fs_rev, N)
        f = convSr[n-1:2*n-1]
        # For p=0 S[0]=0, no issue.
        grad += lin - (b + f)
    grad *= dx
    return grad
```

Objective fast:
```
def fast_full_c_full(h):
  n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
  N=pow2
  F=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
  ac=np.fft.irfft(F*np.conj(F), N)[:n]
  prefix=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(h)))
  p=np.arange(n)
  c_plus=prefix[n-p]-ac
  c_minus=prefix[n]-prefix[p]-ac
  full=np.empty(2*n-1)
  full[n-1]=c_plus[0]
  if n>1:
    full[:n-1]=c_plus[1:][::-1]
    full[n:]=c_minus[1:]
  return full*dx, full (maybe unscaled)
```
Note `prefix[n-p]`: p array int. Good. For p=0 prefix[n]; p=n-1 prefix[1].

```
def smooth_descent_refine(h, n, deadline, alpha_schedule, max_inner_time=None):
  h=project_box_sum(h)
  best=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy(); initial=best
  lr = 0.2
  for alpha in alpha_schedule:
    if elapsed > deadline: break
    sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.time()+ 60?)
    while time<sub_deadline:
      c=fast_c5(h)
      w=softmax(alpha*c)
      g=grad_smooth(h,n,w)
      gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
      if gn<1e-12: break
      # gradient direction normalized; we want step not too large.
      # Determine initial step from current c spread:
      # since objective scale unit, choose step = min(1.0, 1.0/(gn+1e-8)) maybe too big.
      base = 0.5 / (gn + 1e-8)
      base = max(1e-5, min(0.5, base))
      improved=False
      step=base
      for _ in range(12):
         y=h-step*g
         hn=project_box_sum(y)
         cfast=fast_c5(hn)
         if cfast < best - 1e-12:
            ce=exact_c5(hn) if cfast < best - 1e-10 else cfast
            if ce < best - 1e-12:
               h=hn; best=ce; besth=hn.copy(); improved=True; break
            else:
               # fast false, try smaller
               pass
         step*=0.6
      if not improved:
         # try smaller fixed steps maybe 1e-3 to 1e-6
         for sp in [0.05,0.01,0.002,0.0005,1e-4]: ...
         if not improve:
             # random smooth perturbation of small size to escape? Maybe break alpha
             # Cap iterations no_improve 20 break
```

```
def lse_value_grad(c, alpha):
   cmax=c.max()
   s=alpha*(c-cmax)
   mx=s.max()
   se=np.exp(s-mx)
   val = cmax + (mx + np.log(se.sum()))/alpha  # mx=0
   w = se/se.sum()
   return val, w
```

```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p < m: p <<= 1
    return p
```
Could cache in dict? Top-level `_N_CACHE={}` allowed? Yes. Use mutable global within function? It's top-level function can modify global dict; no closures. But no FS. Fine.
```
def _fft_len(n):
    key=n
    if key not in _FFT_CACHE: _FFT_CACHE[key]=_pow2(2*n-1)
    return _FFT_CACHE[key]
```

Projection:
```
def project_box_sum(h, target=None):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    n=len(h)
    if target is None: target=n*0.5
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
       h=np.zeros(n); h[:int(target)]=1.0? But not.
    if n==0: return h
    lo=min(float(np.min(h))-1.0, -1.0)
    hi=max(float(np.max(h))+1.0, 1.0)
    # target clamp
    if target <=0: return np.zeros(n)
    if target >=n: return np.ones(n)
    for _ in range(70):
       tau=0.5*(lo+hi)
       z=np.clip(h-tau,0.0,1.0)
       s=float(z.sum())
       if s > target:
           lo=tau
       else:
           hi=tau
    tau=0.5*(lo+hi)
    z=np.clip(h-tau,0.0,1.0)
    diff=target - z.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
       if diff > 0:
          # add to coordinate with largest room, if cannot, distribute
          idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-z))
          room=1.0-z[idx]
          if room > 0: z[idx]=min(1.0,z[idx]+diff)
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmin(z))
          room=z[idx]
          if room > 0: z[idx]=max(0.0,z[idx]+diff)
    # second fix
    diff=target-z.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-10:
       # scale free variables
       free=((z>1e-14)&(z<1-1e-14))
       if np.any(free):
          z[free] += diff / free.sum()
       else:
          # distribute to saturated? adjust one
       z=np.clip(z,0,1)
    # final exact by iterative small adjust? Use water fill again on z?
    z=project_box_sum(z,target)? recursion? Avoid.
    # Use another bisection but no final adjust
    lo = float(np.min(z))-1.0; hi=float(np.max(z))+1.0
    for _ in range(50):
      tau=0.5*(lo+hi); zz=np.clip(z-tau,0,1); s=zz.sum()
      if s > target: lo=tau
      else: hi=tau
    z=np.clip(z-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    return z
```
Could recursion? no. Simpler: do 100 bisection and return; residual negligible. But exact sum maybe required? Task says sum(h)*dx=1 equivalently sum(h) == n/2 exactly.
```
diff=target-z.sum()
if diff !=0:
  idx=np.argmax(1-z) if diff>0 else np.argmin(z)
  z[idx]=np.clip(z[idx]+diff,0,1)
  # If clipped, distribute residual among other free variables
  diff=target-z.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-12:
     free=np.where((z>0)&(z<1))[0]
     if free.size:
        z[free]+=diff/free.size
```

Fast c functions:
```
def fast_c_full(h):
    n=len(h); N=_fft_len(n); dx=2.0/n
    F=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
    ac=np.fft.irfft(F*np.conj(F), N)[:n]
    # Clean small imaginary not, irfft real. Maybe ac = np.real(ac)
    prefix=np.empty(n+1); prefix[0]=0.0; np.cumsum(h, out=prefix[1:])
    p=np.arange(n)
    cplus=prefix[n-p]-ac
    cminus=prefix[n]-prefix[p]-ac
    full=np.empty(2*n-1)
    full[n-1]=cplus[0]
    if n>1:
       full[:n-1]=cplus[1:][::-1]
       full[n:]=cminus[1:]
    # remove negative tiny? Maybe full=np.maximum(full,0.0) but max could be affected? tiny negative harmless.
    return full*dx
```
Using `prefix[n-p]` where p np array, okay.
For n=1? target 0.5 impossible? But n likely >1. handle n=1: full length1 = h*(1-h)*2? `cplus=prefix[1]-ac=h-h^2; full dx*...` fine.

`fast_c5(h)` returns `float(np.max(fast_c_full(h)))`.

Exact C:
```
def exact_c5(h):
  n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
  return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')) * dx)
```
Could be slightly different due FFT. Use for best.

Smooth value/weights:
```
def softmax_weights(c, alpha):
   if c.size==0: return c.copy()
   cmax=float(c.max())
   s=alpha*(c-cmax)
   # max_s=0
   Ex=np.exp(s) # since cmax max, s<=0, no overflow
   ssum=Ex.sum()
   if ssum <=0 or not np.isfinite(ssum):
       # active mask max
       w=np.zeros_like(c)
       w[np.isclose(c,cmax,rtol=0,atol=1e-15)]=1.0
       w/=w.sum()
       return w
   return Ex/ssum
```
Since s≤0, exp in [0,1] no overflow. Good.
`smooth_value(c, alpha)`:
```
   s=alpha*(c-cmax); Ex=np.exp(s); L = cmax + math.log(Ex.sum())/alpha
```
If Ex.sum=0 (underflow all except maybe max? Ex for max=1, sum≥1 if alpha finite? Actually for cmax, s=0 exp=1, so sum>=1). Good.

Gradient from weights:
```
def gradient_smooth(h, n, w):
   dx=2.0/n
   c0w=w[n-1]
   grad=(1.0-2.0*h)*c0w
   if n>1:
      L=np.zeros(n); R=np.zeros(n)
      # left p
      L[1:] = w[n-2::-1] # check syntax
      R[1:] = w[n:]
      S=L+R
      total_L = L[1:].sum()
      cum_L = np.empty(n); cum_L[0]=0.0; if n>1: cum_L[1:]=np.cumsum(L[1:])
      suffix_L=total_L-cum_L
      cum_R = np.empty(n); cum_R[0]=0.0; cum_R[1:]=np.cumsum(R[1:])
      lin = suffix_L + cum_R
      N=_fft_len(n)
      Fh=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
      Fs=np.fft.rfft(S,N)
      b=np.fft.irfft(Fh*Fs,N)[:n]
      Sr=np.fft.irfft(Fh*np.fft.rfft(S[::-1],N),N)
      f=Sr[n-1:2*n-1]
      grad += lin - (b+f)
   return grad*dx
```
Need avoid operations when n=1. For n=1, full length1 c0 = h(1-h)dx; grad = (1-2h)*c0w*dx; but w[0] sum weights=1. Good.
Check `L[1:] = w[n-2::-1]`: For n=5, w indices left 0,1,2,3. `w[3::-1]` yields 3,2,1,0 length4; assigned L[1..4] -> p1 w3 good. For n=2, `w[0::-1]` length1; L[1]=w0. Good. If n-2 negative? n=1 skip.
`R[1:] = w[n:]`: for n=5 w[5:9] p1..4. Good.

Maybe weights sum to 1 but due c scaled dx; weights independent.

`gradient_smooth_max(h,n,alpha)` calls c, weights, gradient.

1. `lbfgs_penalty_refine(h,n,deadline, alpha_list, mu=5.0, maxiter...)`:
```
def lbfgs_penalty_refine(h, n, deadline, alpha_list=None, mu=5.0, max_time_per=60.0):
    h=project_box_sum(h)
    best=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
    if alpha_list is None: alpha_list=[50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000]
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    for alpha in alpha_list:
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       t_start=time.time()
       # maxiter estimate
       maxiter=max(20, int(2 * (min(max_time_per, deadline-time.time())*20)))? 
       try:
          res=minimize(_lbfgs_obj, h, jac=_lbfgs_grad, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-10, 'maxfun': maxiter*10, 'maxls':40, 'disp':False})
       except: pass
       h2=project_box_sum(res.x)
       c2=exact_c5(h2)
       if c2<best-1e-12: best=besth=...; h=h2
    return h,best
```
Need `res` may fail; use h if res None? minimize returns opt. If n large, bounds list creation cost. Could pass bounds as tuple? okay.
`_lbfgs_obj` and `_lbfgs_grad` top-level. They call fast_c_full, softmax weights. Sum penalty. Also if x outside [0,1] due numerical, clip? But objective with x outside maybe. Bounds should keep. Use `x=np.clip(x,0,1)` in obj/grad? If we clip, gradient discontinuity. But harmless early? Better not, or check.
```
def _lbfgs_obj(x,n,alpha,mu):
   x = np.asarray(x,float)
   c=fast_c_full(x)
   cmax=c.max()
   s=alpha*(c-cmax)
   val = cmax + math.log(np.exp(s).sum())/alpha
   val += mu*(x.sum()-n/2.0)**2
   return float(val)
```
Since s<=0, exp sum >=1. Sum target `n*0.5`.
Grad:
```
def _lbfgs_grad(x,n,alpha,mu):
   c=fast_c_full(x)
   w=softmax_weights(c,alpha)
   g=gradient_smooth(x,n,w)
   g += 2*mu*(x.sum()-n*0.5)*np.ones(n)
   return g
```
`gradient_smooth` returns gradient of C (with dx). Penalty grad of `mu*(sum-target)^2` = 2mu*diff*ones. Good.
Potential: `fast_c_full` uses FFT with x possibly outside [0,1], c may have negatives, but okay.

But L-BFGS-B may evaluate many times. To control time, option `maxiter` based. Maybe not time-checked internally; if one call too long, budget break after. For n=1000, 200 iterations each 2 FFT ~ maybe 0.5s? okay. maxiter 100.

2. `projected_gradient_refine(h,n,deadline, alphas, exact_every?)`:
Implement manually to directly use exact fast. We can use `fast_c_full` each iter. Projected gradient with line search.
Pseudo:
```
def projected_gradient_refine(h, n, deadline, alphas=None, step_base=0.2, max_noimprove=30, sub_deadline_sec=60):
   h=project_box_sum(h); best=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
   if alphas is None: alphas=[20,50,100,500,2000,10000,50000]
   dx=2/n
   for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       # run until sub_deadline or no improve
       t_phase_start=time.time()
       noimprove=0
       while time.time() < deadline and time.time()-t_phase_start < 35:
          c=fast_c_full(h)
          w=softmax_weights(c,alpha)
          g=gradient_smooth(h,n,w)
          gn=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
          if gn < 1e-13: break
          # initial step: ensure not too large: maybe step = 0.5/(1+gn)???
          step=max(1e-5, min(1.0, 0.2*(best+0.1)/max(gn,1e-8)?) )
          improved=False
          # We can use step candidates base and halves. Since h is bounded projection, step large may jump.
          for sp in (0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,1e-4):
             y=h-sp*g
             hn=project_box_sum(y)
             if not np.any(np.abs(hn-h)>1e-15): continue
             cfast=fast_c5(hn)
             if cfast < best - 1e-12:
                ce=exact_c5(hn)
                if ce < best - 1e-12:
                   h=hn; best=ce; besth=hn.copy(); improved=True; break
          if not improved:
             noimprove +=1
             # try tiny fixed moves? maybe break to next alpha
             if noimprove > 3:
                # small random smooth perturb to escape
                # but in deterministic function maybe rng? Could use seed from h? maybe not.
                break
       # after phase, if no substantial improvement, maybe continue
   return besth,best
```
Fixed step candidates ignore gradient norm; may be too large. Better `steps=[]; s=0.5; for _ in range(10): steps.append(min(s,0.5/(gn+?)))`? Let's design.
When g norm is ~1, step 0.1 reasonable. When g norm small, step bigger. Use `smax = 1.0/(gn+1e-8); smax=min(1.0, max(smax,0.05));` steps = smax * [1,0.5,0.25,...] but not less than 1e-6. If gnorm huge (active many), smax 0.01. Good.
```
smax = 1.0/(gn+1e-8); smax = min(1.0, max(smax, 0.05? maybe don't max if huge conv))
```
If gn=10, smax=0.1; if gn=0.01, smax=100 cap1. But if gn small, gradient direction tiny; step 1 maybe. Use cap 0.5.
Could also scale step by current objective margin? not.

But after projection, objective may increase because exact max non-smooth. Accept if decreases. If no decrease, maybe try moving opposite? no.

We can use smooth objective line search to get direction, but exact cfast may not decrease due inactive lags. If no step works, alpha maybe too low; increase alpha. Good.

However line search evaluating `fast_c5` for 8 steps every iteration expensive. If each iteration no improve, break. Fine.

Could use exact subgradient of active set weights uniform: same.

3. Pair local refine:
Use gradient to identify promising pairs. Let's implement `pair_gradient_refine(h,n,deadline,K=60,deltas_base=None)`.
```
def pair_gradient_refine(h,n,deadline,K=40, rounds=...):
  h=project_box_sum(h); best=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
  alphas=[1000,10000,100000]
  for alpha in alphas:
    for _ in range(20? while time):
       c=fast_c_full(h); w=softmax_weights(c,alpha); g=gradient_smooth(h,n,w)
       order=np.argsort(g)
       low=order[:K]; high=order[-K:][::-1]
       improved=False
       for j in high:
         if time... break
         hj=h[j]
         if hj <=1e-12: continue
         for i in low:
            if i==j: continue
            if 1-h[i] <=1e-12: continue
            # possible delta max
            dmax=min(hj,1-h[i])
            # initial delta based on gradient diff
            gd = g[j]-g[i]
            if gd <=0: continue
            # scale: step= min(dmax*0.5, 0.1*gd/(1+np.max(gmag)))? 
            delta = min(0.25*dmax, 0.05*gd/(np.max(np.abs(g))+1e-12)) maybe
            if delta < 1e-6: delta = min(1e-4,0.01*dmax)
            # Try delta and halves
            dd=delta
            for _ in range(6):
               if dd < 1e-8: break
               h[i]+=dd; h[j]-=dd
               cfast=fast_c5(h)
               if cfast < best - 1e-12:
                  ce=exact_c5(h)
                  if ce < best - 1e-12:
                     best=ce; besth=h.copy(); improved=True; break
               h[i]-=dd; h[j]+=dd
               dd*=0.5
            if improved and time>...: break? Actually keep scanning same h updated to improve.
         if improved: continue
         else: break
       if not improved: break
  return besth,best
```
But updating h in nested loops and then continuing with old g; okay. To avoid O(K^2) huge: K=40 ->1600 pairs, each 6 evaluations=9600 fast evals per round. n=600 fast O(600 log) ~0.01? 96k? maybe 0.5-1s. Multiple rounds okay. Use K=30.
Maybe use exact c5 only when fast improves. Good.
Potential issue modifying h[i],h[j] in loops breaks pair symmetry and may invalidate later in same round but accepted. Fine.
Can include deltas absolute [1e-3,5e-4,1e-4] if gradient diff not available. But gradient difference might be tiny. Use log-scale candidate deltas relative to dmax: `[0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.01,0.001]*dmax`, choose first improve. This independent of gradient and may find descents of exact max. For K=30, 1800*6=10800.
Maybe use gradient-defined max delta: if gd ==0 tiny, skip. Good.

Could use top pairs by gradient difference product with room? Use scoring `score=(g[j]-g[i])*min(h[j],1-h[i])`? K fixed.

4. Multi-start perturbation refine:
```
def make_perturbed(h, rng, amp, low_freq=True):
  n=len(h); x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2/n
  noise=0
  for k in range(1,5):
    a=rng.uniform(-1,1); b=rng.uniform(-1,1)
    noise += (a*np.cos(np.pi*k*x) + b*np.sin(np.pi*k*x))
  hnew=h + amp*noise/2
  return project_box_sum(hnew)
```
But frequencies need integer over length 2: cos(π k x /2?) x length 2, period 2/k: cos(π k x). Good.
Also add local Gaussian noise: h + amp*rng.randn(n), then smooth with `np.convolve` small kernel. Project.

Run restarts:
- For best_h, for amp in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]: generate, short refine (LBFGS 1 alpha + projected few sec), update.

At end tau root. Good.

Now, exact sum after projection: Bisection gives sum maybe off by 1e-15; one element adjust. But adjusting one element can break exact if diff > room. We can if diff > room, distribute. Implement robust:
```
def _fix_sum(z,target,n):
  diff=target-z.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-12:
    if diff>0:
      # add to those with room proportional to 1-z
      free=np.where(z<1.0-1e-15)[0]
      if free.size:
         room=(1-z[free])
         # if diff <= room.sum(): distribute proportional
         if diff < room.sum():
            z[free]+=diff*room/room.sum()
         else:
            # saturate some?
            z[free]=1.0
            diff=target-z.sum()
            # still impossible if target>n? not.
    else similar
    z=np.clip(z,0,1)
    diff=target-z.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       free=np.where((z>0)&(z<1))[0]
       if free.size: z[free]+=diff/free.size
       else:
          idx=np.argmax(z) if diff<0 else np.argmin(z); z[idx]=np.clip(z[idx]+diff,0,1)
  return z
```
Given small diff, fine.

But if we call project_box_sum after every gradient step, bisection 70*N each step; for n=1000, 70k clip ops, maybe heavy. But projected gradient line search already 8 projections per iter. 70*1000=70k, 8*maybe 100=56M, okay. Could reduce bisection to 50. For feasibility, 50 enough (range 2 -> 1.8e-15). Use 60.

Euclidean projection can be done more efficiently by sorting y and finding tau, but bisection okay. Sorting O(n log n) similar. For many calls, bisection vectorized C? `np.clip` and sum 60 times. n=1000 -> 60k, 1000 calls ->60M, okay. But L-BFGS doesn't project internally; only after. Projected gradient projects many. def fast_project_feasible(h,target):
```
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  for 100:
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
    if diff>0:
       free=h<1-1e-12; if not break; room=1-h[free]; h[free]+=diff*room[free]/room.sum()
    else:
       free=h>1e-12; if not break; h[free]+=diff*h[free]/h.sum(free)
  fix residual
```

Implementation in code:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_CACHE = {}
def _fft_len(n):
   if n in _CACHE: return _CACHE[n]
   m=2*n-1; p=1
   while p<m: p<<=1
   _CACHE[n]=p
   return p
```
Need top-level no globals? Global dict okay.

Projection:
```
def project_box_sum(h, target=None):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0: return h
    if target is None: target = 0.5 * n
    if target <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
    if target >= n: return np.ones(n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
       h[:] = 0.0
    lo = float(np.min(h)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(h)) + 1.0
    # ensure bound enough
    for _ in range(60):
       tau = 0.5*(lo+hi)
       z = np.clip(h - tau, 0.0, 1.0)
       s = float(z.sum())
       if s > target:
          lo = tau
       else:
          hi = tau
    z = np.clip(h - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(z.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
       if diff > 0:
          free = np.where(z < 1.0)[0]
          if free.size:
             room = 1.0 - z[free]
             if diff < room.sum():
                z[free] += diff * (room/room.sum())
             else:
                z[free] = 1.0
                diff = target - z.sum()
       else:
          free = np.where(z > 0.0)[0]
          if free.size:
             mass = z[free]
             if -diff < mass.sum():
                z[free] += diff * (mass/mass.sum())
             else:
                z[free] = 0.0
                diff = target - z.sum()
       z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
       diff = target - float(z.sum())
       if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
          free = np.where((z > 0.0) & (z < 1.0))[0]
          if free.size:
             z[free] += diff / free.size
          else:
             if diff > 0:
                idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - z))
             else:
                idx = int(np.argmin(z))
             z[idx] += diff
          z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
    return z
```
Maybe after final clip diff not exact. Could do loop twice. To ensure exact, implement a final `while abs(diff)>...` but limit. A robust function `_enforce_sum(z,target)` that loops 10:
```
for _ in range(10):
  diff=target-z.sum()
  if abs(diff)<=1e-12: break
  if diff>0:
     idx=np.argmax(1-z)
     add=min(diff,1-z[idx]); z[idx]+=add
  else: idx=np.argmin(z); sub=min(-diff,z[idx]); z[idx]-=sub
z=np.clip(z,0,1)
```
If diff > room one, next iteration. For small diff, one enough. But if exact target integer and z all binary, diff zero. Good.
Add this final loop.

Fast c:
```
def fast_c_full(h):
    n = h.size
    N = _fft_len(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(F * np.conjugate(F), N)[:n]
    # maybe zero small negative
    prefix = np.empty(n+1); prefix[0]=0.0; np.cumsum(h, out=prefix[1:])
    p = np.arange(n)
    cplus = prefix[n-p] - ac
    cminus = prefix[n] - prefix[p] - ac
    full = np.empty(2*n-1)
    full[n-1] = cplus[0]
    if n > 1:
       full[:n-1] = cplus[1:][::-1]
       full[n:] = cminus[1:]
    return full * (2.0 / n)
```
Potential issue: `prefix[n-p]` where p int array. For n=1 p=[0], prefix[1] ok. `prefix[p]` prefix[0]. Good.
Could avoid c++ copy by returning c. Good.

`fast_c5(h): c=fast_c_full(h); return float(c.max())`

Exact:
```
def exact_c5(h):
   n=h.size; return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')) * (2.0/n))
```

Weights:
```
def softmax_weights(c, alpha):
   cmax = float(np.max(c))
   s = alpha * (c - cmax)
   e = np.exp(s)  # s <=0; okay
   se = float(e.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0:
       w=np.zeros(c.size)
       mask = np.isclose(c,cmax,rtol=0,atol=1e-14)
       w[mask]=1.0
       w /= w.sum()
       return w
   return e/se
```
If alpha very large and c-cmax values like -1e-16, alpha* diff underflows for nonactive; e has 1 for active. sum ok.

Smooth value maybe not needed except L-BFGS. Implement:
```
def _smooth_lse(c, alpha):
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    s=alpha*(c-cmax)
    e=np.exp(s)
    return cmax + math.log(float(e.sum()))/alpha
```
Since cmax max, s max 0, e sum>=1. Good.

Gradient:
```
def gradient_from_weights(h, n, w):
    dx=2.0/n
    c0w = w[n-1]
    grad = (1.0 - 2.0*h) * c0w
    if n > 1:
       L = np.zeros(n)
       R = np.zeros(n)
       L[1:] = w[n-2::-1]
       R[1:] = w[n:]
       S = L + R
       cumL = np.empty(n); cumL[0]=0.0
       if n > 1: cumL[1:] = np.cumsum(L[1:])
       suffixL = cumL[-1] - cumL
       cumR = np.empty(n); cumR[0]=0.0
       if n > 1: cumR[1:] = np.cumsum(R[1:])
       lin = suffixL + cumR
       N = _fft_len(n)
       Fh = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
       b = np.fft.irfft(Fh * np.fft.rfft(S, N), N)[:n]
       f = np.fft.irfft(Fh * np.fft.rfft(S[::-1], N), N)[n-1:2*n-1]
       grad += lin - (b + f)
    return grad * dx
```
Check cumL[-1] = total_L = sum L[1:]. Good. cumR length n: cumR[i]=sum R[1..i]. For i=n-1 total. Good.
Potential issue: `w[n:]` length n-1, assign R[1:] length n-1. Good. For n=2, `w[n-2::-1]` = `w[0::-1]` length1. Good.

`gradient_smooth_max(h,n,alpha)`: c=fast_c_full, w=softmax, return gradient.

Penalty L-BFGS functions:
```
def _lbfgs_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    c = fast_c_full(x)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    s = alpha * (c - cmax)
    e = np.exp(s)
    val = cmax + math.log(float(e.sum())) / alpha
    diff = float(np.sum(x) - 0.5*n)
    return float(val + mu * diff * diff)
```
If x out of [0,1] but bounds, fine. If alpha large, s negative, exp sum maybe 1+ small. log finite.
Grad:
```
def _lbfgs_grad(x,n,alpha,mu):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    c=fast_c_full(x)
    w=softmax_weights(c,alpha)
    g=gradient_from_weights(x,len(x),w)
    diff=float(np.sum(x)-0.5*n)
    if diff != 0:
       g = g + (2.0*mu*diff) * np.ones(n)
    return g
```
Potential memory: np.ones each call; could preallocate? okay.
Need ensure if x sum huge diff penalty huge not assert. okay.

`lbfgs_penalty_refine`:

```
def lbfgs_penalty_refine(h, n, deadline, alphas=None, mu=0.0, max_alpha_calls=...??:
    h = project_box_sum(h)
    best = exact_c5(h); besth = h.copy()
    if alphas is None: alphas=[50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000]
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)] * n
    # choose maxiter based on remaining and n
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time() >= deadline or deadline - time.time() < 5: break
       # Estimate iterations: each iteration maybe 2 obj/grad. Use at most 150 for n<=600, 80 for n>1500
       time_left = deadline - time.time()
       maxiter = 80 if n > 1200 else (150 if n <= 600 else 100)
       # But not spend all; cap phase time 120s maybe. We can't pass callback; minimize may run long. To be safe, maxiter low: 60 for first, 30 later.
       # Use maxiter = max(20, min(maxiter, int(3*time_left)))? If time_left 300, 300 iterations maybe.
       # Let's set per_alpha_time = min(60, time_left*0.25). maxiter = int(maxiter * per_alpha_time / 60)
       per_alpha_time = min(60.0, time_left * 0.25)
       maxit = max(20, int(maxiter * per_alpha_time / 60.0))
       try:
          res = minimize(_lbfgs_obj, h, jac=_lbfgs_grad, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxit, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxfun': maxit*10, 'maxls':40, 'disp':False})
          x = res.x
       except Exception:
          continue
       h2 = project_box_sum(x)
       c2 = exact_c5(h2)
       if c2 < best - 1e-12:
          best = c2; besth = h2; h = h2.copy()
       # maybe if no improve after high alpha break?
    return besth, best
```

Projected gradient refine:
```
def projected_gradient_refine(h, n, deadline, alphas=None, phase_time=60.0, max_no_improve=8):
    h=project_box_sum(h); best=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
    if alphas is None: alphas=[100,500,2000,10000,50000,200000]
    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() >= deadline: break
        t_phase = time.time()
        no_improve=0
        while time.time() < deadline and (time.time()-t_phase) < phase_time:
            c=fast_c_full(h)
            w=softmax_weights(c,alpha)
            g=gradient_from_weights(h,n,w)
            gn=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
            if gn < 1e-13: break
            smax = 1.0/(gn + 1e-8)
            smax = min(1.0, max(0.02, smax))  # maybe if gn large 0.1
            improved=False
            step=smax
            for _ in range(10):
                if step < 1e-8: break
                y = h - step * g
                hn = project_box_sum(y)
                cfast = float(np.max(fast_c_full(hn)))
                if cfast < best - 1e-12:
                   ce = exact_c5(hn)
                   if ce < best - 1e-12:
                      h=hn; best=ce; besth=hn.copy(); improved=True; break
                step *= 0.5
            if not improved:
                # try some absolute small steps maybe 1e-3... but g may point wrong
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve >= max_no_improve:
                    break
                # optional: increase alpha next loop automatically
        # after each alpha, maybe if time
    return besth, best
```
Issue: If objective doesn't decrease for step sequence, maybe even tiny step should, unless gradient not descent for exact max. For non-smooth, a subgradient direction may not be descent if not exact subgradient? At maximum, subgradient directions include convex combinations of active; -g is descent only if using subgradient of max convex? But max of bilinear not convex, subgradient condition not sufficient. Smooth gradient may not be descent. So no improvement possible with small steps. Then need pair/random.

`phase_time` default maybe 30 per alpha. We'll set in run.

Pair gradient refine:
```
def pair_gradient_refine(h, n, deadline, K=30, alphas=(2000,10000,50000), max_rounds=20):
    h=project_box_sum(h); best=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
    for alpha in alphas:
      if time.time()>=deadline: break
      for _ in range(max_rounds):
        if time.time()>=deadline: break
        c=fast_c_full(h); w=softmax_weights(c,alpha); g=gradient_from_weights(h,n,w)
        order=np.argsort(g)
        low=order[:K]
        high=order[-K:][::-1]
        improved=False
        gmag=float(np.max(np.abs(g))) + 1e-12
        for j in high:
          if time.time() >= deadline: break
          hj=h[j]
          if hj <= 1e-12: continue
          for i in low:
             if i==j: continue
             room=1.0-h[i]
             if room <= 1e-12: continue
             dmax = min(float(hj), float(room))
             gd = float(g[j]-g[i])
             # if gd small, use base small delta
             delta = min(0.25*dmax, 0.2 * gd / gmag) if gd > 0 else min(0.05, 0.01*dmax)
             if delta < 1e-6: delta = min(1e-4, 0.01*dmax, 1e-3)
             # try fractions: delta, delta/2, delta/4, delta/8, 0.01*dmax, 0.001*dmax
             cands=[]
             for f in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1):
                dd=f*delta
                if dd>0 and dd<dmax and dd not dup: add
             for f in (0.05,0.01,0.001): ...
             for dd in cands:
                h[i]+=dd; h[j]-=dd
                # sum preserved
                cfast=fast_c5(h)
                if cfast < best - 1e-12:
                   ce=exact_c5(h)
                   if ce < best - 1e-12:
                      best=ce; besth=h.copy(); improved=True
                      break
                h[i]-=dd; h[j]+=dd
             if improved:
                # maybe update gradient? break out to recompute? Could break high loop after some improvements to avoid too many.
                # To allow many improvements, continue with old g but h changed. Re-evaluate next outer round if no more.
                # We can break after a wave? For simplicity, continue all pairs; too costly. We'll set if improved and maybe count 10 improvements break round.
                improved_count +=1? Need variable.
                if improved_count >= 10: break
          if time.time()>=deadline or improved_count >=10: break
        if not improved:
           break
    return besth,best
```
Need not overcomplicate. Use `round_improvements=0` and break after 20 improvements per round, recompute gradient.

Candidate deltas need not duplicate; use list then unique by approximate. Simpler:
```
fracs=[0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.01,0.001]
for f in fracs:
  dd=f*dmax
  if dd <1e-8: continue
  ...
```
This ignores gradient but safe. Starting with 0.5*dmax may be too big (no improve). But line search halves. For each pair 6 evals. K=30 -> 5400 fast evals per round; okay. Could reduce K=20 -> 2400. For high n, maybe. Use K=25.
Use accept if exact improves. Since after improvement high/low order stale; but small. Break after 8 improvements to recompute.

Maybe use direct `fast_c5` not `fast_c_full` for pair; OK.

```
def fourier_perturb(h, rng, amp):
   n=len(h)
   x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=True)
   noise=np.zeros(n)
   for k in range(1,6):

       noise += a*np.cos(np.pi*k*x/2?)
```

Implement:
```
def refine_candidate(h, n, deadline, alloc):
   h = project_box_sum(h)
   if h.size != n:
       h = resample_h(h,n); h=project_box_sum(h)
   c = exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
   t_end = min(deadline, time.time()+alloc)
   # LBFGS budget 0.5 alloc, maybe minimum 20
   t1 = min(t_end, time.time()+max(20.0, 0.5*alloc))
   if time.time() < t1 - 10:
      h1,c1 = lbfgs_penalty_refine(h,n,t1, alphas=[100,500,2000,10000,50000,200000], mu=1.0)
      if c1<bestc: update h=...
   # projected budget 0.25 alloc
   t2 = min(t_end, time.time()+max(15.0,0.25*alloc))
   h2,c2=projected_gradient_refine(h,n,t2, alphas=[500,2000,10000,50000], phase_time=20)
   # pair budget 0.2 alloc
   t3 = min(t_end, time.time()+max(10.0,0.2*alloc))
   h3,c3=pair_gradient_refine(h,n,t3,K=25,alphas=[5000,20000,100000],max_rounds=10)
   # sym
   hs,cs=try_symmetrize(h)
   if cs<c update
   return besth,bestc
```

Let's think of KKT spectral method. The smooth max gradient's quadratic part is Toeplitz with weights S. The gradient condition for unconstrained (interior, no sum) is `(1-2h)w0 + lin - (h convolved S) = 0`. This is linear in h! For fixed weights w (i.e., λ), the gradient of smooth max with that fixed λ? Wait gradient of Fλ is linear in h: ∇Fλ = b + A h. Setting to constant (plus sum multiplier) gives linear system! Actually Fλ is quadratic, gradient is affine in h. We can solve for h satisfying KKT for weighted sum. Then update λ = softmax of resulting c? This is a majorization algorithm. Interesting!

Define for a fixed weight vector λ (sum 1) over lags, Fλ(h)=∑ λ_m c_m is a quadratic (with constant Hessian). Its gradient is affine: ∇Fλ(h)=Aλ h + bλ, which our `gradient_from_weights` without dx? Actually `gradient_from_weights` returns ∑ w_m ∇c_m, which is exactly gradient of weighted sum F_w (since weights fixed), and it is affine in h. We can solve the equation (for interior with sum constraint) ∇F_w(h) = λ_sum * 1 (constant) and h∈[0,1]. This is a (possibly indefinite) linear system plus box. If we can solve, we get a stationary point of weighted sum. Then update weights w = softmax(alpha*c(h)). Iterate (Dantzig-Wolfe-like). This may converge to fixed point of smooth max (KKT). This is different from gradient descent and could improve.

How solve ∇F_w(h)=γ 1? ∇F_w(h)=dx*( lin - Toeplitz_S*h + w0(1-2h) ). Here lin depends on weights only, not h? Wait lin is sum of weights for linear parts, independent of h. Yes. The Toeplitz term S*h (with f+b) is linear in h. So gradient = const(age weights) - M h where M is symmetric Toeplitz-like with S off-diagonal plus 2w0 on diagonal? Specifically `grad/dx = lin + w0 - 2w0 h - (f+b)`. f+b = (T_S h)_i with T_S having offdiag S[|i-j|]. So ∇ = (lin + w0) - (2w0 I + T_S) h. Set = γ (times dx ignored). So (2w0 I + T_S) h = (lin + w0) - γ. We need also sum h = n/2. This is a linear system. The matrix M = 2w0 I + Toeplitz(S) (with offdiag S[p]). S are nonnegative weights sums. M is diagonally dominant? Row sum of offdiag maybe. If M is positive definite, solve easily. Even if indefinite, can use `np.linalg.lstsq` or conjugate gradient if SPD. S distances from softmax nonneg; Toeplitz with arbitrary nonneg S may have negative eigenvalues if high S? Matrix with positive diagonal and positive offdiagonal? Offdiag S>0, not negative. Implement `toeplitz_apply(v,S)`:
```
def toeplitz_apply(v,S):
 n=len(v); N=_fft_len(n); Fv=np.fft.rfft(v,N)
 b=np.fft.irfft(Fv*np.fft.rfft(S,N),N)[:n]
 f=np.fft.irfft(Fv*np.fft.rfft(S[::-1],N),N)[n-1:2*n-1]
 return b+f
```
This is exactly f+b. `apply_M(v,S,w0,rho)` returns `toeplitz_apply(v,S) + (2*w0+rho)*v`.
In gradient, `g = (lin + w0) - M h`. Let's verify: M h = 2w0? Wait gradient formula had grad/dx = (1-2h)*w0 + lin - (f+b) = w0 + lin - (2w0 h + (f+b)). So M = 2w0 I + T_S, yes.

Implement `newton_active_refine` top-level:
```
def newton_active_refine(h,n,deadline,alphas=(1000,5000,20000,100000),rho=3.0,maxcg=30,phase_time=20.0):
    h=project_box_sum(h)
    best=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time()>=deadline: break
       t_phase=time.time()
       no_improve=0
       while time.time()<deadline and time.time()-t_phase < phase_time:
          c=fast_c_full(h)
          w=softmax_weights(c,alpha)
          w0=float(w[n-1])
          S=np.zeros(n)
          if n>1:
             S[1:] = w[n-2::-1] + w[n:]
          g=gradient_from_weights(h,n,w)
          dx=2.0/n
          g_nox=g/dx
          rhs = g_nox - np.mean(g_nox)
          rn=np.linalg.norm(rhs)
          if rn < 1e-12: break
          z=np.zeros(n)
          r=rhs.copy()
          p=r.copy()
          rr=float(np.dot(r,r))
          solved=False
          for it in range(maxcg):
             Ap=apply_M(p,S,w0,rho)
             pAp=float(np.dot(p,Ap))
             if pAp <= 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(pAp): break
             alpha_cg=rr/pAp
             z += alpha_cg*p
             r -= alpha_cg*Ap
             rr_new=float(np.dot(r,r))
             if math.sqrt(rr_new) < 1e-8*rn:
                solved=True; break
             p = r + (rr_new/rr)*p
             rr=rr_new
          delta = z - np.mean(z)
          if np.linalg.norm(delta) < 1e-12: break
          improved=False
          for step in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125,1.5,2.0? maybe 1 first):
              hn=project_box_sum(h + step*delta)
              cfast=fast_c5(hn)
              if cfast < best-1e-12:
                 ce=exact_c5(hn)
                 if ce<best-1e-12:
                    h=hn; best=ce; besth=hn.copy(); improved=True; break
          if not improved:
             no_improve +=1
             if no_improve >= 4: break
    return besth,best
```
`apply_M` top-level:
```
def apply_M(v,S,w0,rho):
   return 2.0*w0*v + toeplitz_apply(v,S) + rho*v
```

```
def toeplitz_apply_pref(v, Fs, Fsr):
   Fv=np.fft.rfft(v, len(Fs))
   b=np.fft.irfft(Fv*Fs, len(Fs))[:len(v)]
   f=np.fft.irfft(Fv*Fsr, len(Fs))[len(v)-1:2*len(v)-1]
   return b+f
```
In newton, compute Fs,Fsr once per alpha (S fixed). CG matvec uses pref. Also gradient calculation already uses S? gradient_from_weights recomputes; could also use pref but okay. For each phase, S fixed; CG 30 matvecs * (1 Fv 2 ifft) + initial gradient. Good.

```
def apply_M_pref(v,w0,rho,Fs,Fsr):
   n=len(v)
```

`_fft_len`:
```
def _fft_len(n):
    m = max(1, 2*n - 1)
    p=1
    while p < m: p <<= 1
    return p
```
No cache? Could add cache dict. Top-level `_FFT_CACHE = {}`. Static dict okay.
```
def _fft_len(n):
  v=_FFT_CACHE.get(n)
  if v is None: ... _FFT_CACHE[n]=p
  return p
```
No issue.

`resample_h`:
```
def resample_h(h, n_new):
    n_old=len(h)
    if n_old == n_new: return h.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old,endpoint=True)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new,endpoint=True)
    y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h,left=h[0],right=h[-1])
    return project_box_sum(y,n_new*0.5)
```
Maybe if `h` not finite, fallback. Fine.

`project_box_sum` final sum loop:
Implement robust:
```
def project_box_sum(h, target=None):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    n=h.size
    if n==0: return h
    if target is None: target=0.5*n
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
       h[:] = 0.5
    if target <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
    if target >= n: return np.ones(n)
    lo=float(np.min(h))-1.0; hi=float(np.max(h))+1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       tau=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=float(np.clip(h-tau,0.0,1.0).sum())
       if s > target: lo=tau
       else: hi=tau
    z=np.clip(h-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(20):
       diff=target-float(z.sum())
       if abs(diff) <= 1e-13: break
       if diff > 0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-z))
          add=min(diff, 1.0-z[idx])
          z[idx] += add
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmin(z))
          sub=min(-diff, z[idx])
          z[idx] -= sub
    z=np.clip(z,0.0,1.0)
    # if still not, distribute
    diff=target-float(z.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
       if diff > 0:
          free=np.where(z < 1.0 - 1e-15)[0]
          if free.size:
             room=1.0-z[free]
             if diff < room.sum():
                z[free] += diff * (room/room.sum())
          else: z[:] = min(1.0, z+diff/n?) no
       else:
          free=np.where(z > 1e-15)[0]
          if free.size:
             mass=z[free]
             if -diff < mass.sum():
                z[free] += diff * (mass/mass.sum())
       z=np.clip(z,0,1)
    return z
```
The one-by-one loop can take up to 20 if diff large and one coordinate room insufficient. But after bisection diff tiny, one iteration. Good. If target beyond feasible due nan? No.

`lbfgs_penalty_refine` details:
```
def lbfgs_penalty_refine(h,n,deadline,alphas=None,mu=1.0):
    h=project_box_sum(h)
    best=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
    if alphas is None:
       if n <=500: alphas=[100,500,2000,10000,50000,200000]
       elif n <=1000: alphas=[100,1000,10000,50000]
       else: alphas=[200,2000,20000,100000]
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    max_base=160 if n<=400 else (100 if n<=800 else (70 if n<=1200 else 40))
    for ai,alpha in enumerate(alphas):
       time_left=deadline-time.time()
       if time_left<5: break
       rem=len(alphas)-ai
       per_alpha=min(45.0, time_left/max(1,rem))
       maxit=int(max(15, min(max_base, 8*per_alpha))) # if per_alpha 30 ->240 cap base; small 5 ->40? Actually 8*5=40 min15.
       try:
          res=minimize(_lbfgs_obj, h, jac=_lbfgs_grad, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-11,'maxfun':maxit*10,'maxls':30,'disp':False})
       except Exception:
          continue
       x=getattr(res,'x',None)
       if x is None: continue
       h2=project_box_sum(x)
       c2=exact_c5(h2)
       if c2 < best-1e-12:
          best=c2; besth=h2.copy(); h=h2.copy()
       # if res.success? not important
    return besth,best
```
`8*per_alpha` for alpha with 45s maybe 360 cap base. For n=300 max 160 per alpha; 6 alphas 960 iterations maybe okay? Each fast. Could be too much but time check after each alpha not inside. A single `minimize` with 160 iters may take? For n=300, maybe 1-2s? 6=10s. Fine. For n=1200 max 70*4=280 iters; each maybe 10ms =>3s. Good. Actually L-BFGS line search extra fun; maybe 20ms =>6s. Good.

But if `minimize` with 160 iterations for n=300 maybe each 1ms? Anyway.

Potential problem: L-BFGS-B with bounds and penalty may not respect target sum exactly, but final project. Good.

`newton_active_refine` code with top-level `toeplitz_apply_pref`: need `N=len(Fs)` in helper? We can use `len(Fv)`. Implementation:
```
def toeplitz_apply_pref(v, Fs, Fsr):
    n=v.size
    N=Fs.size
    Fv=np.fft.rfft(v,N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(Fv*Fs,N)[:n]
    f=np.fft.irfft(Fv*Fsr,N)[n-1:2*n-1]
    return b+f
```
Ensure `2*n-1 <= N`: N>=2n-1. Good.
`apply_M_pref`:
```
def apply_M_pref(v, w0, rho, Fs, Fsr):
    return (2.0*w0 + rho)*v + toeplitz_apply_pref(v,Fs,Fsr)
```
`newton_active_refine`:
```
def newton_active_refine(h,n,deadline,alphas=None,rho=2.5,maxcg=35,phase_time=25.0):
   h=project_box_sum(h); best=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
   if alphas is None:
      if n<=500: alphas=[1000,5000,20000,100000]
      else: alphas=[2000,10000,50000]
   dx=2.0/n
   for alpha in alphas:
     if time.time()>=deadline: break
     t_phase=time.time(); no=0
     while time.time()<deadline and time.time()-t_phase < phase_time:
       c=fast_c_full(h)
       w=softmax_weights(c,alpha)
       w0=float(w[n-1])
       S=np.zeros(n)
       if n>1:
          S[1:]=w[n-2::-1] + w[n:]
       g=gradient_from_weights(h,n,w)/dx
       gm=float(np.mean(g))
       rhs=g-gm
       rn=float(np.linalg.norm(rhs))
       if rn < 1e-11: break
       N=_fft_len(n)
       Fs=np.fft.rfft(S,N); Fsr=np.fft.rfft(S[::-1],N)
       z=np.zeros(n); r=rhs.copy(); p=r.copy(); rr=float(np.dot(r,r)); base=1e-7*rn
       for _ in range(maxcg):
          Ap=apply_M_pref(p,w0,rho,Fs,Fsr)
          pAp=float(np.dot(p,Ap))
          if pAp <= 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(pAp): break
          a=rr/pAp
          z += a*p
          r -= a*Ap
          rr_new=float(np.dot(r,r))
          if math.sqrt(rr_new) < base:
             break
          p = r + (rr_new/rr)*p
          rr=rr_new
       delta=z - float(np.mean(z))
       dn=float(np.linalg.norm(delta))
       if dn < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(dn): break
       improved=False
       for step in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,1.5,0.0625,2.0,0.03125):
          if time.time() >= deadline: break
          hn=project_box_sum(h + step*delta)
          cfast=float(np.max(fast_c_full(hn)))
          if cfast < best - 1e-12:
             ce=exact_c5(hn)
             if ce < best - 1e-12:
                h=hn; best=ce; besth=hn.copy(); improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          no +=1
          if no >=4: break
   return besth,best
```
Potential: `S[::-1]` when n=1 length1. Fine.
Segmentation: `w[n-2::-1]` for n=1 skip.

Could improve line search by using `fast_c_full` max instead of `fast_c5` function? fine.

`projected_gradient_refine`: Maybe less needed if newton. Keep for safety.
```
def projected_gradient_refine(h,n,deadline,alphas=None,phase_time=25.0,max_no_improve=5):
   h=project_box_sum(h); best=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
   if alphas is None: ...
   for alpha in alphas:
     t_phase=time.time(); no=0
     while time.time()<deadline and time.time()-t_phase<phase_time:
       c=fast_c_full(h); w=softmax_weights(c,alpha); g=gradient_from_weights(h,n,w)
       gn=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
       if gn<1e-12: break
       smax=1.0/(gn+1e-8); smax=min(1.0,max(0.02,smax))
       step=smax; improved=False
       for _ in range(10):
          if step<1e-8 or time.time()>=deadline: break
          hn=project_box_sum(h-step*g)
          cfast=fast_c5(hn)
          if cfast < best-1e-12:
             ce=exact_c5(hn)
             if ce < best-1e-12:
                h=hn; best=ce; besth=hn.copy(); improved=True; break
          step*=0.5
       if not improved:
          no++; if no>=max_no_improve: break
   return besth,best
```
Maybe if `step*s` large projection to bounds. Good.

`pair_gradient_refine`:
```
def pair_gradient_refine(h,n,deadline,K=None,alphas=(5000,20000,100000),max_rounds=8):
   h=project_box_sum(h); best=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
   if K is None:
      K=25 if n<=600 else (12 if n<=1200 else 8)
   if max_rounds is None: selected.
   for alpha in alphas:
     if time.time()>=deadline: break
     rounds=0
     while time.time()<deadline and rounds<max_rounds:
       rounds +=1
       c=fast_c_full(h); w=softmax_weights(c,alpha); g=gradient_from_weights(h,n,w)
       order=np.argsort(g)
       low=order[:K]
       high=order[-K:][::-1]
       improved=0
       # To avoid duplicate pairs, if K large and low/high overlap. Use set? We'll just check i!=j.
       for j in high:
          if time.time()>=deadline: break
          hj=float(h[j])
          if hj <= 1e-13: continue
          for i in low:
             if time.time()>=deadline: break
             if i==j: continue
             room=1.0-float(h[i])
             if room <= 1e-13: continue
             dmax=min(hj,room)
             # Try fractions of dmax
             for fr in (0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.01,0.002):
               dd=fr*dmax
               if dd <=1e-9: continue
               h[i]+=dd; h[j]-=dd
               cfast=fast_c5(h)
               if cfast < best-1e-12:
                  ce=exact_c5(h)
                  if ce < best-1e-12:
                     best=ce; besth=h.copy(); improved +=1; break
               h[i]-=dd; h[j]+=dd
             if improved >= 12:
                break
          if improved >=12: break
       if improved ==0: break
   return besth,best
```
After improvement, h changed but high/low fixed. Break after 12 to recompute. Good.
Potential issue: When improving, we don't restore? We accept and leave h modified. For subsequent pairs in same round, high/low old but h changed; may still okay. For non-accepted, restored. Sum preserved; after accepted, sum unchanged. Good.
Could use `fast_c5` many times; exact only when fast improves. Good.

`fourier_perturb`:
```
def fourier_perturb(h, rng, amp):
   n=len(h); x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=True)
   noise=np.zeros(n)
   for k in range(1,7):
      a=rng.uniform(-1,1); b=rng.uniform(-1,1)
      noise += a*np.cos(np.pi*k*x) + b*np.sin(np.pi*k*x)
   noise /= max(1.0, float(np.max(np.abs(noise))))
   # add small gaussian lowpass
   g=rng.randn(n)
   kernel=np.ones(min(11, max(3,n//20))) / ...?
   if n>5:
      klen=min(11, n if n%2==1 else n-1)
      if klen <3: klen=1
      kernel=np.ones(klen)/klen
      g=np.convolve(g, kernel, mode='same')
   noise = 0.6*noise + 0.4*g/ (max(1,np.max(abs(g))) if np.any else 1)
   return project_box_sum(h + amp*noise)
```
Top-level. Need avoid lambdas.

But random perturb may make h sum off; project.

`refine_candidate`:
```
def refine_candidate(h, n, deadline, alloc):
    h = project_box_sum(h)
    if h.size != n:
       h = resample_h(h,n)
    c = exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
    t_end = min(deadline, time.time()+alloc)
    # LBFGS
    t1 = min(t_end, time.time() + max(15.0, 0.40*alloc))
    if time.time() < t1 - 5:
       h1,c1 = lbfgs_penalty_refine(h,n,t1,mu=1.0)
       if c1 < c? Use best: if c1<bestc update
    # newton
    t2 = min(t_end, time.time() + max(10.0, 0.25*alloc))
    if time.time() < t2 - 5:
       h2,c2=newton_active_refine(h,n,t2)
       update
    # projected
    t3 = min(t_end, time.time()+max(8.0,0.15*alloc))
    if time.time() < t3 - 3:
       h3,c3=projected_gradient_refine(h,n,t3)
       update
    # pair
    t4 = min(t_end, time.time()+max(5.0,0.20*alloc))
    if time.time() < t4 - 2:
       h4,c4=pair_gradient_refine(h,n,t4)
       update
    # sym
    if time.time() < t_end:
       hs,cs=try_symmetrize(h); if cs<c update h=hs
    h=project_box_sum(h)
    c=exact_c5(h)
    return h,c
```

`quick_refine` implementation:
```
def quick_refine(h,n,deadline,alloc):
    h=project_box_sum(h)
    if h.size != n: h=resample_h(h,n)
    c=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
    t_end=min(deadline,time.time()+alloc)
    t1=min(t_end,time.time()+max(5,0.5*alloc))
    if time.time()<t1-3:
       h1,c1=lbfgs_penalty_refine(h,n,t1, alphas=[2000,10000] if n>800 else [1000,5000,20000], mu=1.0)
       update
    t2=min(t_end,time.time()+max(4,0.3*alloc))
    if time.time()<t2-2:
       h2,c2=newton_active_refine(h,n,t2, alphas=[10000] if n>800 else [5000,20000], phase_time=10)
       update
    t3=min(t_end,time.time()+max(2,0.2*alloc))
    if time.time()<t3-1:
       h3,c3=pair_gradient_refine(h,n,t3,K=10,alphas=[20000],max_rounds=3)
       update
    return besth,best_c
```

Detailed function code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_FFT_CACHE = {}

def _fft_len(n):
    n = int(n)
    p = _FFT_CACHE.get(n)
    if p is not None:
        return p
    m = max(1, 2 * n - 1)
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    _FFT_CACHE[n] = p
    return p
```

`project_box_sum`: Need if `h` is scalar? `np.array` gives shape; use `h = np.atleast_1d`? If h list. `h = np.array(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)` to 1D.
```
def project_box_sum(h, target=None):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size == 0: return h.copy()
    h = h.copy()
    n=h.size
    if target is None: target=0.5*n
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): h[:] = 0.5
    if target <= 0.0: return np.zeros(n)
    if target >= float(n): return np.ones(n)
    lo = float(np.min(h)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(h)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(45): ...
```
If h all 0 and target 0.5n, root tau=-0.5? At lo=-1, hi=1. bisection. okay.
Inside final loop, `np.argmax(1.0-z)` if diff>0. If all z=1 and diff>0 impossible target>n. If all z=0 diff<0 target<0 impossible. Good.
Potential if after bisection `z.sum()` nan? no.

`evenize`:
```
def evenize(n):
    n=int(n)
    if n < 2: return 2
    return n if n % 2 == 0 else n + 1
```

`resample_h`:
```
def resample_h(h, n_new):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n_old = h.size
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return project_box_sum(h, 0.5*n_new)
    if n_old == 0: return project_box_sum(np.full(n_new,0.5),0.5*n_new)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
    return project_box_sum(y, 0.5*n_new)
```
Use endpoint=True. If n_new=1, target .5, project maybe sets 0.5? Clip? bisection target .5, y single. okay.

`fast_c_full`:
```
def fast_c_full(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0: return np.empty(0)
    N = _fft_len(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(F * np.conjugate(F), N)[:n]
    prefix = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    prefix[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(h, out=prefix[1:])
    p = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    cplus = prefix[n - p] - ac
    cminus = prefix[n] - prefix[p] - ac
    full = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    full[n - 1] = cplus[0]
    if n > 1:
        full[:n - 1] = cplus[1:][::-1]
        full[n:] = cminus[1:]
    return full * (2.0 / float(n))
```
Check `prefix[n - p]` with p int array; `n - p` array. Good.
For n=1, full[0]=cplus[0], if n>1 skip.

`fast_c5`:
```
def fast_c5(h):
    c = fast_c_full(h)
    if c.size == 0: return 0.0
    return float(np.max(c))
```

`exact_c5`:
```
def exact_c5(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n=h.size
    if n==0: return 0.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / float(n)))
```
If c maybe negative? max. good.

`softmax_weights`:
```
def softmax_weights(c, alpha):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    m = c.size
    if m == 0: return c.copy()
    if alpha is None or alpha <= 0 or not np.isfinite(alpha):
        return np.full(m, 1.0/m)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.full(m,1.0/m)
    s = alpha * (c - cmax)
    e = np.exp(s)
    se = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
       w=np.zeros(m)
       mask = (c >= cmax - 1e-15)
       w[mask]=1.0
       ws=float(np.sum(w))
       if ws<=0: w[0]=1.0
       else: w/=ws
       return w
    w=e/se
    return w
```

`gradient_from_weights`:
Need use `w` length full (2n-1). If not, maybe uniform. For safety:
```
def gradient_from_weights(h, n, w):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(n)
    if n != h.size: h = h[:n] if h.size>n else np.pad(h,(0,n-h.size))
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    m = 2*n - 1
    if w.size != m:
       # create uniform
       w = np.full(m, 1.0/m)
    ws = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0: w = np.full(m,1.0/m)
    else: w = w/ws
    dx = 2.0/float(n)
    w0 = float(w[n-1])
    grad = (1.0 - 2.0*h) * w0
    if n > 1:
       L = np.zeros(n, dtype=float); R = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
       L[1:] = w[n-2::-1]
       R[1:] = w[n:]
       S = L + R
       cumL = np.empty(n, dtype=float); cumL[0]=0.0
       if n > 1: cumL[1:] = np.cumsum(L[1:])
       suffixL = cumL[-1] - cumL
       cumR = np.empty(n, dtype=float); cumR[0]=0.0
       if n > 1: cumR[1:] = np.cumsum(R[1:])
       lin = suffixL + cumR
       N = _fft_len(n)
       Fh = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
       b = np.fft.irfft(Fh * np.fft.rfft(S, N), N)[:n]
       f = np.fft.irfft(Fh * np.fft.rfft(S[::-1], N), N)[n-1:2*n-1]
       grad += lin - (b + f)
    return grad * dx
```
If n=0? not. If n=1, skip S, but `N` not used. Good.
`w[n-2::-1]` for n=2 okay. For n=1 skip. For n>1, `w[n:]` length n-1. Good.
`S[::-1]` if S length n. Good.

`_lbfgs_obj` and grad:
```
def _lbfgs_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    c=fast_c_full(x)
    if c.size==0: val=0.0
    else:
       cmax=float(np.max(c))
       if alpha and alpha>0 and np.isfinite(alpha):
          s=alpha*(c-cmax)
          val=cmax + math.log(float(np.sum(np.exp(s))))/alpha
       else: val=cmax
    diff=float(np.sum(x) - 0.5*float(n))
    val += float(mu)*diff*diff
    return float(val)
```
If x length not n? minimum passes n. okay.
```
def _lbfgs_grad(x,n,alpha,mu):
   x=...
   c=fast_c_full(x)
   w=softmax_weights(c,alpha)
   g=gradient_from_weights(x,n,w)
   diff=float(np.sum(x)-0.5*n)
   if diff !=0:
      g = g + (2.0*mu*diff)*np.ones(n)
   return g
```
If n mismatch? okay.

`lbfgs_penalty_refine`:
Need `bounds` for n length. If n huge memory okay. Use `res` maybe `res.x`. If `res.x` None? Use `if not hasattr(res,'x')`. Good.
```
def lbfgs_penalty_refine(h, n, deadline, alphas=None, mu=1.0):
    h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5*n).copy()
    best = exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
    if alphas is None:
       if n <= 400: alphas=[100,500,2000,10000,50000,200000]
       elif n <= 900: alphas=[200,1000,5000,20000,100000]
       else: alphas=[500,2000,10000,50000]
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] * n
    if n <= 400: base=120
    elif n <= 800: base=80
    elif n <= 1200: base=55
    else: base=35
    for ai,alpha in enumerate(alphas):
       tl = deadline - time.time()
       if tl < 5: break
       rem = max(1, len(alphas)-ai)
       per = min(40.0, tl/float(rem))
       maxit = int(max(12, min(base, 6.0*per)))
       try:
          res = minimize(_lbfgs_obj, h, jac=_lbfgs_grad, args=(n,alpha,mu), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxit, 'ftol': 1e-14, 'gtol': 1e-11, 'maxfun': max(20, maxit*4), 'maxls': 20, 'disp': False})
       except Exception:
          continue
       x = getattr(res, 'x', None)
       if x is None: continue
       h2 = project_box_sum(x, 0.5*n)
       c2 = exact_c5(h2)
       if c2 < best - 1e-12:
          best = c2; besth = h2.copy(); h = h2.copy()
    return besth, best
```
`per` if time left large but only few alphas; cap 40 means maxit maybe base if per>base/6. For base 120, per 40 ->720 cap 120. Good. If time left 40, rem 6, per6 ->36 maxit; cap base. Fine.

`toeplitz_apply_pref`:
```
def toeplitz_apply_pref(v, Fs, Fsr):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n=v.size; N=Fs.size
    Fv=np.fft.rfft(v,N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(Fv*Fs,N)[:n]
    f=np.fft.irfft(Fv*Fsr,N)[n-1:2*n-1]
    return b+f
```
If N from _fft_len(n), Fs size N. Good. But if `N` is not enough for f slice `2*n-1`? `_fft_len` ensures ≥2n-1. Fs built with same. Good.

`apply_M_pref`:
```
def apply_M_pref(v, w0, rho, Fs, Fsr):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    return (2.0*float(w0) + float(rho))*v + toeplitz_apply_pref(v,Fs,Fsr)
```

`newton_active_refine`:
Use defaults alphas based on n. Need `math.sqrt` maybe if rr_new negative due roundoff? rr_new dot nonnegative but could negative tiny; use max(0).
```
def newton_active_refine(h, n, deadline, alphas=None, rho=2.5, maxcg=35, phase_time=None):
    h=project_box_sum(h,0.5*n).copy()
    best=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
    if alphas is None:
       if n <= 500: alphas=[1000,5000,20000,100000]
       else: alphas=[3000,10000,40000]
    if phase_time is None: phase_time = 20.0 if n > 900 else 30.0
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       t_phase = time.time(); no=0
       while time.time() < deadline and (time.time()-t_phase) < phase_time:
           c=fast_c_full(h)
           w=softmax_weights(c, alpha)
           w0=float(w[n-1])
           S=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
           if n > 1:
              S[1:] = w[n-2::-1] + w[n:]
           g = gradient_from_weights(h, n, w) / dx
           gm = float(np.mean(g))
           rhs = g - gm
           rn = float(np.linalg.norm(rhs))
           if rn < 1e-11: break
           N = _fft_len(n)
           Fs = np.fft.rfft(S, N)
           Fsr = np.fft.rfft(S[::-1], N)
           z = np.zeros(n)
           r = rhs.copy()
           p = rhs.copy()
           rr = float(np.dot(r,r))
           tol = 1e-7 * rn
           for _ in range(maxcg):
              Ap = apply_M_pref(p, w0, rho, Fs, Fsr)
              pAp = float(np.dot(p, Ap))
              if pAp <= 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(pAp): break
              a = rr / pAp
              z += a*p
              r -= a*Ap
              rr_new = float(np.dot(r,r))
              if rr_new < 0.0: rr_new = 0.0
              if math.sqrt(rr_new) < tol:
                  break
              p = r + (rr_new / rr) * p
              rr = rr_new
              if rr <= 0.0: break
           delta = z - float(np.mean(z))
           dn = float(np.linalg.norm(delta))
           if dn < 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(dn): break
           improved=False
           for step in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 1.5, 0.125, 2.0, 0.0625, 3.0):
              if time.time() >= deadline: break
              hn = project_box_sum(h + step*delta, 0.5*n)
              cfast = float(np.max(fast_c_full(hn)))
              if cfast < best - 1e-12:
                 ce = exact_c5(hn)
                 if ce < best - 1e-12:
                    h = hn; best = ce; besth = hn.copy(); improved=True; break
           if not improved:
              no += 1
              if no >= 4: break
    return besth, best
```
Potential: `S[::-1]` copy. Good.
If `n` maybe 0? no.
`phase_time` if deadline close, while check. Good.

`projected_gradient_refine` similar. Use `fast_c5` for cfast. Need avoid `np.linalg.norm` if n large? okay.
```
def projected_gradient_refine(h,n,deadline,alphas=None,phase_time=None,max_no_improve=5):
   h=project_box_sum(h,0.5*n).copy()
   best=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
   if alphas is None:
      if n<=500: alphas=[500,2000,10000,50000]
      else: alphas=[2000,10000,40000]
   if phase_time is None: phase_time = 15.0 if n>900 else 25.0
   for alpha in alphas:
      if time.time()>=deadline: break
      t_phase=time.time(); no=0
      while time.time()<deadline and (time.time()-t_phase)<phase_time:
          c=fast_c_full(h); w=softmax_weights(c,alpha)
          g=gradient_from_weights(h,n,w)
          gn=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
          if gn < 1e-12: break
          smax = 1.0/(gn+1e-8)
          smax = min(1.0, max(0.02, smax))
          step = smax; improved=False
          for _ in range(10):
             if step < 1e-8 or time.time()>=deadline: break
             hn=project_box_sum(h - step*g, 0.5*n)
             cfast=fast_c5(hn)
             if cfast < best - 1e-12:
                ce=exact_c5(hn)
                if ce < best - 1e-12:
                   h=hn; best=ce; besth=hn.copy(); improved=True; break
             step *= 0.5
          if not improved:
             no += 1
             if no >= max_no_improve: break
   return besth,best
```
Line search if step=1 and g norm small? smax capped 1. If gnorm small, direction tiny, step 1 may be huge relative; projection ok. If no improve, halves. Good.

`pair_gradient_refine`:
```
def pair_gradient_refine(h,n,deadline,K=None,alphas=None,max_rounds=None):
   h=project_box_sum(h,0.5*n).copy()
   best=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy()
   if K is None:
      K=24 if n <= 600 else (12 if n <= 1200 else 8)
   if alphas is None:
      alphas = [5000,20000,100000] if n <= 1000 else [10000,50000]
   if max_rounds is None:
      max_rounds = 10 if n <= 600 else 6
   for alpha in alphas:
      if time.time()>=deadline: break
      rounds=0
      while time.time()<deadline and rounds < max_rounds:
         rounds += 1
         c=fast_c_full(h); w=softmax_weights(c,alpha); g=gradient_from_weights(h,n,w)
         order=np.argsort(g)
         low=order[:K]
         high=order[-K:][::-1]
         improved=0
         for j in high:
            if time.time()>=deadline: break
            hj=float(h[j])
            if hj <= 1e-13: continue
            for i in low:
               if time.time()>=deadline or improved >= 12: break
               if i == j: continue
               room = 1.0 - float(h[i])
               if room <= 1e-13: continue
               dmax = min(hj, room)
               for fr in (0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.01,0.002):
                  dd = fr*dmax
                  if dd <= 1e-9: continue
                  h[i] += dd; h[j] -= dd
                  cfast=fast_c5(h)
                  if cfast < best - 1e-12:
                     ce=exact_c5(h)
                     if ce < best - 1e-12:
                        best=ce; besth=h.copy(); improved += 1; break
                  h[i] -= dd; h[j] += dd
         if improved == 0:
            break
   return besth,best
```
If accepted modification, after `break` out of for fr, h remains modified. Then next i/j uses modified h. The `low` and `high` indices continue. If later non-accepted, we restore from current h, okay. Improved count. If `h[i]` and `h[j]` cross? dmax ensures. After accepted, for next cands, dmax from modified. Good.

Potential bug: If accepted and `break` inner fr, then after fr loop, continue i. But if `improved>=12` checked at top of i. Good.

```
def try_symmetrize(h):

   return hs, exact_c5(hs)
```

```
def try_shifts(h):

      c=exact_c5(hs)
      if c<bestc:
         besth=hs; bestc=c
   return besth,bestc
```

```
def try_regularize(h):
   h=project_box_sum(h); n=h.size
   if n<5: return h, exact_c5(h)
   hs=np.empty(n)
   hs[0]=0.7*h[0]+0.3*h[1]
   hs[-1]=0.7*h[-1]+0.3*h[-2]
   hs[1:-1]=0.4*h[1:-1]+0.3*h[:-2]+0.3*h[2:]
   hs=project_box_sum(hs)
   return hs, exact_c5(hs)
```

`fourier_perturb`:
```
def fourier_perturb(h, rng, amp):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n=h.size
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    noise=np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1,8):
       amp_k = rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)
       ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi)
       noise += amp_k * np.cos(np.pi*k*x + ph)
    maxn=float(np.max(np.abs(noise)))
    if maxn > 1e-12: noise /= maxn
    # add low-freq random phase already. Maybe add random linear gradient
    noise += 0.5 * (x/2.0 - 0.5) * rng.uniform(-1,1)
    maxn=float(np.max(np.abs(noise)))
    if maxn > 1e-12: noise /= maxn
    return project_box_sum(h + amp*noise)
```
No lambdas.

`refine_candidate`:
```
def refine_candidate(h,n,deadline,alloc):
    n=int(n)
    h=project_box_sum(h,0.5*n)
    if h.size != n:
       h=resample_h(h,n)
    c=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy(); bestc=c
    t_end=min(deadline, time.time()+float(alloc))
    # LBFGS
    t1=min(t_end, time.time()+max(10.0,0.40*alloc))
    if time.time() < t1 - 3:
       h1,c1=lbfgs_penalty_refine(h,n,t1,mu=1.0)
       if c1 < bestc:
          bestc=c1; besth=h1.copy(); h=h1.copy()
    # Newton
    t2=min(t_end, time.time()+max(8.0,0.25*alloc))
    if time.time() < t2 - 2:
       h2,c2=newton_active_refine(h,n,t2)
       if c2 < bestc: update
    # projected
    t3=min(t_end, time.time()+max(5.0,0.15*alloc))
    if time.time() < t3 - 1:
       h3,c3=projected_gradient_refine(h,n,t3)
       update
    # pair
    t4=min(t_end, time.time()+max(4.0,0.20*alloc))
    if time.time() < t4 - 1:
       h4,c4=pair_gradient_refine(h,n,t4)
       update
    # sym/shift/regularize if time
    if time.time() < t_end - 2:
       hs,cs=try_symmetrize(h); update if lower
       if time.time() < t_end - 2:
          hs,cs=try_shifts(h); update
       if time.time() < t_end - 2:
          hs,cs=try_regularize(h); update
    h=project_box_sum(besth,0.5*n)
    c=exact_c5(h)
    return h,c
```
If `alloc` large, t1 maybe t_end? We set `t1=min(t_end, time+0.4alloc)`. After phase, time may be > t1, t2=min(t_end,time+0.25alloc) > t1, okay. Sum fractions 1.0. If phase overshoots due minimize, t_end prevents further but time could exceed next. Conditions check.
Need if no improvement but faster phases consumed less, remaining time still t_end? t2 uses time+0.25alloc, not all t_end, so unused time from LBFGS not used by newton? Actually if LBFGS returns early, time nearer t_start; t2 = time+0.25alloc, so we don't use leftover from LBFGS until pair? At end t_end not fully used. Could set t2 = min(t_end, time + max(8,0.25*alloc + (t1-time)?)) complicated. But okay. To use more, in run final can call refine again leftover. Reserve final handles.

`quick_refine` similar, use fractions.

`run`: Need ensure no variable name conflict. Let's write carefully.
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + budget - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        h0 = np.full(250, 0.5)
    if h0.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(h0)):
        h0 = np.full(250, 0.5)
    n0 = int(h0.size)
    best_h = project_box_sum(h0, 0.5*n0)
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h)
    best_n = n0
    remaining = deadline - time.time()
    if remaining < 20.0:
        return best_h, float(best_c), best_n
    # candidates
    max_n = 1200
    if n0 > max_n:
       cands = [n0]
    else:
       raw = [n0, evenize(600), evenize(3*n0), evenize(1200), evenize(4*n0)]
       cands=[]
       for nraw in raw:
          n = int(nraw)
          if n < 20: continue
          if n > max_n and n != n0: n = max_n if max_n %2==0 else max_n-1
          if n not in cands:
             cands.append(n)
       if n0 not in cands:
          cands.insert(0,n0)
       nonbase = sorted(set(cands) - {n0})
       if len(nonbase) > 2:
          selected = [nonbase[0], nonbase[-1]]
       else:
          selected = nonbase
       cands = [n0] + sorted(selected)
       if len(cands) > 3:
          cands = cands[:3]
    total = deadline - time.time()
    reserve_final = min(60.0, max(15.0, 0.05*total))
    reserve_restart = min(90.0, max(25.0, 0.08*total))
    opt_budget = max(0.0, total - reserve_final - reserve_restart)
    if len(cands) == 1: fracs=[1.0]
    elif len(cands) == 2: fracs=[0.40,0.60]
    else: fracs=[0.25,0.35,0.40]
    for idx,ntry in enumerate(cands):
        if time.time() >= deadline - reserve_final - 5.0: break
        alloc = fracs[idx] * opt_budget
        if idx == 0:
            alloc = max(alloc, 20.0)
        else:
            if alloc < 25.0: break
        # If starting h length ntry? best_h current
        h_try, c_try = refine_candidate(best_h, ntry, min(deadline - reserve_final - 5.0, time.time()+alloc), alloc)
        if c_try < best_c:
            best_c = c_try; best_h = h_try; best_n = int(ntry if h_try.size == int(ntry) else h_try.size)
    # restarts
    if time.time() < deadline - reserve_final - 5.0:
       amps = (0.03, 0.07, 0.12)
       for amp in amps:
           if time.time() >= deadline - reserve_final - 5.0: break
           htry = fourier_perturb(best_h, rng, amp)
           alloc = min(30.0, deadline - time.time() - reserve_final - 5.0)
           if alloc < 8.0: break
           ht, ct = quick_refine(htry, best_n, min(deadline - reserve_final - 2.0, time.time()+alloc), alloc)
           if ct < best_c:
              best_c=ct; best_h=ht; best_n=int(ht.size)
    # final polish
    remaining = deadline - time.time()
    if remaining > 10.0:
       hpol, cpol = refine_candidate(best_h, best_n, deadline - 2.0, min(remaining - 2.0, 60.0? or remaining))
       if cpol < best_c: update
    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, 0.5*best_n)
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_h.size)
```

```
def quick_refine(h,n,deadline,alloc):
    n=int(n)
    h=project_box_sum(h,0.5*n)
    if h.size != n: h=resample_h(h,n)
    c=exact_c5(h); besth=h.copy(); bestc=c
    t_end=min(deadline, time.time()+float(alloc))
    t1=min(t_end,time.time()+max(3.0,0.5*alloc))
    if time.time()<t1-1:
       h1,c1=lbfgs_penalty_refine(h,n,t1, alphas=[2000,10000] if n>800 else [1000,5000,20000], mu=1.0)
       if c1<bestc: update
    t2=min(t_end,time.time()+max(2.0,0.3*alloc))
    if time.time()<t2-0.5:
       h2,c2=newton_active_refine(h,n,t2, alphas=[10000] if n>800 else [5000,20000], phase_time=8.0)
       if c2<bestc: update
    t3=min(t_end,time.time()+max(1.0,0.2*alloc))
    if time.time()<t3-0.2:
       h3,c3=pair_gradient_refine(h,n,t3,K=8,alphas=[20000],max_rounds=3)
       update
    h=project_box_sum(besth,0.5*n)
    return h, exact_c5(h)
```

Implementation of active weights:
```
def active_weights(c, tol=None):
   c=np.asarray(c,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
   if c.size==0: return c.copy()
   cmax=float(np.max(c))
   if tol is None: tol=max(1e-12, 1e-10*abs(cmax))
   mask = c >= cmax - tol
   w=np.zeros(c.size)
   w[mask]=1.0
   s=float(w.sum())
   if s<=0: w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
   else: w/=s
   return w
```

```
def _interp(h, n_new, left=False):
   n_old=len(h)
   if left:
      x_old=np.arange(n_old)*(2.0/n_old)
      x_new=np.arange(n_new)*(2.0/n_new)
   else:
      x_old=(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)*(2.0/n_old)
      x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*(2.0/n_new)
   return np.interp(x_new,x_old,h,left=h[0],right=h[-1])
def resample_h(h,n_new):
   n_old=len(h); n_new=int(n_new)
   h=project_box_sum(h,0.5*n_old)
   if n_old == n_new: return h
   y1=project_box_sum(_interp(h,n_new,False),0.5*n_new)
   c1=fast_c5(y1)
   y2=project_box_sum(_interp(h,n_new,True),0.5*n_new)
   c2=fast_c5(y2)
   return y1 if c1 <= c2 else y2
```

Let's write `active_weights`:
```
def active_weights(c, tol_factor=1e-11):
   c=np.asarray(c,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
   m=c.size
   if m==0: return c.copy()
   cmax=float(np.max(c))
   tol=float(tol_factor)*max(1.0,abs(cmax))
   mask = c >= cmax - tol
   w=np.zeros(m)
   w[mask]=1.0
   s=float(np.sum(w))
   if s <=0:
      w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
   else:
      w/=s
   return w
```

```
def smooth_gaussian_noise(rng,n):
   g=rng.randn(n)
   klen=min(21,n)
   if klen%2==0: klen-=1
   if klen>1:
      kernel=np.ones(klen)/klen
      g=np.convolve(g,kernel,mode='same')
   maxg=max(1,np.max(abs(g))); return g/maxg
```

```
def smooth_noise(rng,n):
   n=int(n)
   g=np.random.RandomState? Use rng
   g = rng.randn(n)

   if klen > 1:

      g = np.convolve(g, kernel, mode='same')
   mg=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
```
