```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e100
```
Add in `_lbfgs_obj`, `_lbfgs_grad`? For grad, return ones. But L-BFGS with nan may crash. Add:
```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
   return 1e100
```
Grad similarly. Good.
In fast_c_full, if h nonfinite, FFT nan. Could clip? Add in `_lbfgs_obj`: `if not finite: x=np.clip(x,0,1)`.
```
x=np.asarray(...); if not finite: x=np.clip(np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0),0,1)
```
Use. Good.

In gradient function, if h nonfinite, `gradient_from_weights` maybe. Add at start `h=np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)`. Good.

Now, final code.

Let's ensure no use of `np.random.RandomState` if seed None? seed int default. Good.

Potential issue: `np.convolve` in smooth_noise with n small, mode 'same' returns length n. If klen=1, kernel [1], g unchanged. Good.

Now, final.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_FFT_CACHE = {}


def _fft_len(n):
    n = int(n)
    p = _FFT_CACHE.get(n)
    if p is not None:
        return p
    m = max(1, 2 * n - 1)
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    _FFT_CACHE[n] = p
    return p


def evenize(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return 2
    return n if n % 2 == 0 else n + 1


def project_box_sum(h, target=None):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size == 0:
        return h.copy()
    h = h.copy()
    n = h.size
    if target is None:
        target = 0.5 * n
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        h[:] = 0.5
    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if target >= float(n):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)

    lo = float(np.min(h)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(h)) + 1.0

    for _ in range(45):
        tau = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(h - tau, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = tau
        else:
            hi = tau

    z = np.clip(h - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(30):
        diff = target - float(z.sum())
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - z))
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - z[idx])
            z[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(z))
            sub = min(-diff, z[idx])
            z[idx] -= sub

    z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(z.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0.0:
            free = np.where(z < 1.0 - 1e-15)[0]
            if free.size:
                room = 1.0 - z[free]
                if diff < room.sum():
                    z[free] += diff * (room / room.sum())
                else:
                    z[free] = 1.0
        else:
            free = np.where(z > 1.0e-15)[0]
            if free.size:
                mass = z[free]
                if -diff < mass.sum():
                    z[free] += diff * (mass / mass.sum())
                else:
                    z[free] = 0.0
        z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)

    return z


def fast_c_full(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    N = _fft_len(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(F * np.conjugate(F), N)[:n]

    prefix = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    prefix[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(h, out=prefix[1:])

    p = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    cplus = prefix[n - p] - ac
    cminus = prefix[n] - prefix[p] - ac

    full = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    full[n - 1] = cplus[0]
    if n > 1:
        full[:n - 1] = cplus[1:][::-1]
        full[n:] = cminus[1:]
    return full * (2.0 / float(n))


def fast_c5(h):
    c = fast_c_full(h)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(c))


def exact_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def softmax_weights(c, alpha):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return c.copy()
    if alpha is None or alpha <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(alpha):
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=float)

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=float)

    s = alpha * (c - cmax)
    e = np.exp(s)
    se = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        mask = (c >= cmax - 1.0e-14)
        w[mask] = 1.0
        ws = float(np.sum(w))
        if ws <= 0.0:
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w /= ws
        return w
    return e / se


def active_weights(c, tol_factor=1.0e-11):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return c.copy()
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    tol = float(tol_factor) * max(1.0, abs(cmax))
    mask = c >= (cmax - tol)
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    w[mask] = 1.0
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= s
    return w


def gradient_from_weights(h, n, w):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = int(n)
    if h.size != n:
        if h.size > n:
            h = h[:n]
        else:
            h = np.pad(h, (0, n - h.size), mode='constant')
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)

    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size != m:
        w = np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=float)
    ws = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=float)
    else:
        w = w / ws

    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    w0 = float(w[n - 1])
    grad = (1.0 - 2.0 * h) * w0

    if n > 1:
        L = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        R = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        L[1:] = w[n - 2::-1]
        R[1:] = w[n:]
        S = L + R

        cumL = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
        cumL[0] = 0.0
        cumL[1:] = np.cumsum(L[1:])
        suffixL = cumL[-1] - cumL

        cumR = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
        cumR[0] = 0.0
        cumR[1:] = np.cumsum(R[1:])

        lin = suffixL + cumR

        N = _fft_len(n)
        Fh = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
        b = np.fft.irfft(Fh * np.fft.rfft(S, N), N)[:n]
        f = np.fft.irfft(Fh * np.fft.rfft(S[::-1], N), N)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
        grad += lin - (b + f)

    return grad * dx


def _lbfgs_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)

    c = fast_c_full(x)
    if c.size == 0:
        val = 0.0
    else:
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if alpha is not None and alpha > 0.0 and np.isfinite(alpha):
            s = alpha * (c - cmax)
            esum = float(np.sum(np.exp(s)))
            val = cmax + math.log(esum) / float(alpha)
        else:
            val = cmax

    diff = float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(n))
    val += float(mu) * diff * diff
    return float(val)


def _lbfgs_grad(x, n, alpha, mu):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)

    c = fast_c_full(x)
    w = softmax_weights(c, alpha)
    g = gradient_from_weights(x, n, w)

    diff = float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(n))
    if diff != 0.0:
        g = g + (2.0 * float(mu) * diff) * np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    return g


def lbfgs_penalty_refine(h, n, deadline, alphas=None, mu=1.0):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n).copy()
    best = exact_c5(h)
    besth = h.copy()

    if alphas is None:
        if n <= 400:
            alphas = [100, 500, 2000, 10000, 50000, 200000]
        elif n <= 900:
            alphas = [200, 1000, 5000, 20000, 100000]
        else:
            alphas = [500, 2000, 10000, 50000]

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

    if n <= 400:
        base = 120
    elif n <= 800:
        base = 80
    elif n <= 1200:
        base = 55
    else:
        base = 35

    for ai, alpha in enumerate(alphas):
        tl = deadline - time.time()
        if tl < 5.0:
            break
        rem = max(1, len(alphas) - ai)
        per = min(40.0, tl / float(rem))
        maxit = int(max(12, min(base, 6.0 * per)))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lbfgs_obj,
                h,
                jac=_lbfgs_grad,
                args=(n, alpha, mu),
                method='L-BFGS-B',
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    'maxiter': maxit,
                    'ftol': 1.0e-14,
                    'gtol': 1.0e-11,
                    'maxfun': max(20, maxit * 4),
                    'maxls': 20,
                    'disp': False,
                }
            )
        except Exception:
            continue

        x = getattr(res, 'x', None)
        if x is None:
            continue
        h2 = project_box_sum(x, 0.5 * n)
        c2 = exact_c5(h2)
        if c2 < best - 1.0e-12:
            best = c2
            besth = h2.copy()
            h = h2.copy()

    return besth, best


def _interp_h(h, n_new, left=False):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n_old = h.size
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if n_old == 0:
        return np.full(n_new, 0.5)
    if n_old == 1:
        return np.full(n_new, float(h[0]))

    if left:
        x_old = np.arange(n_old, dtype=float) * (2.0 / float(n_old))
        x_new = np.arange(n_new, dtype=float) * (2.0 / float(n_new))
    else:
        x_old = (np.arange(n_old, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_old))
        x_new = (np.arange(n_new, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_new))

    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])


def resample_h(h, n_new):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n_old = h.size
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n_new)
    if n_old == 0:
        return project_box_sum(np.full(n_new, 0.5), 0.5 * n_new)

    h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n_old)

    y1 = project_box_sum(_interp_h(h, n_new, left=False), 0.5 * n_new)
    c1 = fast_c5(y1)

    y2 = project_box_sum(_interp_h(h, n_new, left=True), 0.5 * n_new)
    c2 = fast_c5(y2)

    return y1 if c1 <= c2 else y2


def toeplitz_apply_pref(v, Fs, Fsr):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = v.size
    N = Fs.size
    Fv = np.fft.rfft(v, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(Fv * Fs, N)[:n]
    f = np.fft.irfft(Fv * Fsr, N)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return b + f


def apply_M_pref(v, w0, rho, Fs, Fsr):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    return (2.0 * float(w0) + float(rho)) * v + toeplitz_apply_pref(v, Fs, Fsr)


def newton_active_refine(h, n, deadline, alphas=None, rho=2.5, maxcg=35, phase_time=None):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n).copy()
    best = exact_c5(h)
    besth = h.copy()

    if alphas is None:
        if n <= 500:
            alphas = [-1, 2000, 10000, 50000]
        else:
            alphas = [-1, 5000, 20000, 80000]

    if phase_time is None:
        phase_time = 20.0 if n > 900 else 30.0

    dx = 2.0 / float(n)

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        t_phase = time.time()
        no_improve = 0

        while time.time() < deadline and (time.time() - t_phase) < phase_time:
            c = fast_c_full(h)
            if alpha is None or alpha <= 0:
                w = active_weights(c)
            else:
                w = softmax_weights(c, alpha)

            w0 = float(w[n - 1])
            S = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            if n > 1:
                S[1:] = w[n - 2::-1] + w[n:]

            g = gradient_from_weights(h, n, w) / dx
            gm = float(np.mean(g))
            rhs = g - gm
            rn = float(np.linalg.norm(rhs))
            if rn < 1.0e-11:
                break

            N = _fft_len(n)
            Fs = np.fft.rfft(S, N)
            Fsr = np.fft.rfft(S[::-1], N)

            z = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            r = rhs.copy()
            p = rhs.copy()
            rr = float(np.dot(r, r))
            tol = 1.0e-7 * rn

            for _ in range(maxcg):
                Ap = apply_M_pref(p, w0, rho, Fs, Fsr)
                pAp = float(np.dot(p, Ap))
                if pAp <= 1.0e-14 or not np.isfinite(pAp):
                    break
                a = rr / pAp
                z += a * p
                r -= a * Ap
                rr_new = float(np.dot(r, r))
                if rr_new < 0.0:
                    rr_new = 0.0
                if math.sqrt(rr_new) < tol:
                    break
                if rr <= 0.0:
                    break
                p = r + (rr_new / rr) * p
                rr = rr_new

            delta = z - float(np.mean(z))
            dn = float(np.linalg.norm(delta))
            if dn < 1.0e-12 or not np.isfinite(dn):
                break

            improved = False
            for step in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 1.5, 0.125, 2.0, 0.0625, 3.0):
                if time.time() >= deadline:
                    break
                hn = project_box_sum(h + step * delta, 0.5 * n)
                cfast = float(np.max(fast_c_full(hn)))
                if cfast < best - 1.0e-12:
                    ce = exact_c5(hn)
                    if ce < best - 1.0e-12:
                        h = hn
                        best = ce
                        besth = hn.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break

            if not improved:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve >= 4:
                    break

    return besth, best


def projected_gradient_refine(h, n, deadline, alphas=None, phase_time=None, max_no_improve=5):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n).copy()
    best = exact_c5(h)
    besth = h.copy()

    if alphas is None:
        if n <= 500:
            alphas = [500, 2000, 10000, 50000]
        else:
            alphas = [2000, 10000, 40000]

    if phase_time is None:
        phase_time = 15.0 if n > 900 else 25.0

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        t_phase = time.time()
        no_improve = 0

        while time.time() < deadline and (time.time() - t_phase) < phase_time:
            c = fast_c_full(h)
            w = softmax_weights(c, alpha)
            g = gradient_from_weights(h, n, w)
            gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
            if gn < 1.0e-12:
                break

            smax = 1.0 / (gn + 1.0e-8)
            smax = min(1.0, max(0.02, smax))
            step = smax
            improved = False

            for _ in range(10):
                if step < 1.0e-8 or time.time() >= deadline:
                    break
                hn = project_box_sum(h - step * g, 0.5 * n)
                cfast = fast_c5(hn)
                if cfast < best - 1.0e-12:
                    ce = exact_c5(hn)
                    if ce < best - 1.0e-12:
                        h = hn
                        best = ce
                        besth = hn.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break
                step *= 0.5

            if not improved:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve >= max_no_improve:
                    break

    return besth, best


def pair_gradient_refine(h, n, deadline, K=None, alphas=None, max_rounds=None):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n).copy()
    best = exact_c5(h)
    besth = h.copy()

    if K is None:
        K = 24 if n <= 600 else (12 if n <= 1200 else 8)
    if alphas is None:
        alphas = [5000, 20000, 100000] if n <= 1000 else [10000, 50000]
    if max_rounds is None:
        max_rounds = 10 if n <= 600 else 6

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        rounds = 0
        while time.time() < deadline and rounds < max_rounds:
            rounds += 1
            h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n)
            c = fast_c_full(h)
            w = softmax_weights(c, alpha)
            g = gradient_from_weights(h, n, w)

            order = np.argsort(g)
            low = order[:K]
            high = order[-K:][::-1]
            improved = 0

            for j in high:
                if time.time() >= deadline:
                    break
                hj = float(h[j])
                if hj <= 1.0e-13:
                    continue
                for i in low:
                    if time.time() >= deadline or improved >= 12:
                        break
                    if i == j:
                        continue
                    room = 1.0 - float(h[i])
                    if room <= 1.0e-13:
                        continue
                    dmax = min(hj, room)
                    for fr in (0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.01, 0.002):
                        dd = fr * dmax
                        if dd <= 1.0e-9:
                            continue
                        h[i] += dd
                        h[j] -= dd
                        cfast = fast_c5(h)
                        if cfast < best - 1.0e-12:
                            ce = exact_c5(h)
                            if ce < best - 1.0e-12:
                                best = ce
                                besth = h.copy()
                                improved += 1
                                break
                        h[i] -= dd
                        h[j] += dd

            if improved == 0:
                break

    return besth, best


def smooth_noise(rng, n):
    n = int(n)
    g = rng.randn(n)
    klen = min(21, n)
    if klen % 2 == 0:
        klen -= 1
    if klen > 1:
        kernel = np.ones(klen, dtype=float) / float(klen)
        g = np.convolve(g, kernel, mode='same')
    mg = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
    if mg > 1.0e-12:
        g /= mg
    return g


def fourier_perturb(h, rng, amp):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n = h.size
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for k in range(1, 8):
        ak = rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        noise += ak * np.cos(np.pi * k * x + ph)
    mg = float(np.max(np.abs(noise)))
    if mg > 1.0e-12:
        noise /= mg

    g = smooth_noise(rng, n)
    noise = 0.6 * noise + 0.4 * g
    noise += 0.4 * (x / 2.0 - 0.5) * rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
    mg = float(np.max(np.abs(noise)))
    if mg > 1.0e-12:
        noise /= mg

    return project_box_sum(h + float(amp) * noise)


def try_symmetrize(h):
    h = project_box_sum(h)
    hs = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
    hs = project_box_sum(hs)
    return hs, exact_c5(hs)


def try_shifts(h):
    h = project_box_sum(h)
    besth = h.copy()
    bestc = exact_c5(h)
    for d in (-0.25, -0.125, -0.05, 0.05, 0.125, 0.25):
        hs = project_box_sum(h + d)
        c = exact_c5(hs)
        if c < bestc:
            bestc = c
            besth = hs
    return besth, bestc


def try_regularize(h):
    h = project_box_sum(h)
    n = h.size
    if n < 5:
        return h, exact_c5(h)
    hs = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    hs[0] = 0.7 * h[0] + 0.3 * h[1]
    hs[-1] = 0.7 * h[-1] + 0.3 * h[-2]
    hs[1:-1] = 0.4 * h[1:-1] + 0.3 * h[:-2] + 0.3 * h[2:]
    hs = project_box_sum(hs)
    return hs, exact_c5(hs)


def quick_refine(h, n, deadline, alloc):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n)
    if h.size != n:
        h = resample_h(h, n)
    c = exact_c5(h)
    besth = h.copy()
    bestc = c

    t_end = min(deadline, time.time() + float(alloc))

    t1 = min(t_end, time.time() + max(3.0, 0.5 * alloc))
    if time.time() < t1 - 1.0:
        if n > 800:
            h1, c1 = lbfgs_penalty_refine(h, n, t1, alphas=[2000, 10000], mu=1.0)
        else:
            h1, c1 = lbfgs_penalty_refine(h, n, t1, alphas=[1000, 5000, 20000], mu=1.0)
        if c1 < bestc:
            bestc = c1
            besth = h1.copy()
            h = h1.copy()

    t2 = min(t_end, time.time() + max(2.0, 0.3 * alloc))
    if time.time() < t2 - 0.5:
        if n > 800:
            h2, c2 = newton_active_refine(h, n, t2, alphas=[-1, 10000], phase_time=8.0)
        else:
            h2, c2 = newton_active_refine(h, n, t2, alphas=[-1, 5000, 20000], phase_time=8.0)
        if c2 < bestc:
            bestc = c2
            besth = h2.copy()
            h = h2.copy()

    t3 = min(t_end, time.time() + max(1.0, 0.2 * alloc))
    if time.time() < t3 - 0.2:
        h3, c3 = pair_gradient_refine(h, n, t3, K=8, alphas=[20000], max_rounds=3)
        if c3 < bestc:
            bestc = c3
            besth = h3.copy()

    h = project_box_sum(besth, 0.5 * n)
    return h, exact_c5(h)


def refine_candidate(h, n, deadline, alloc):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * n)
    if h.size != n:
        h = resample_h(h, n)

    c = exact_c5(h)
    besth = h.copy()
    bestc = c
    t_end = min(deadline, time.time() + float(alloc))

    t1 = min(t_end, time.time() + max(10.0, 0.40 * alloc))
    if time.time() < t1 - 3.0:
        h1, c1 = lbfgs_penalty_refine(h, n, t1, mu=1.0)
        if c1 < bestc:
            bestc = c1
            besth = h1.copy()
            h = h1.copy()

    t2 = min(t_end, time.time() + max(8.0, 0.25 * alloc))
    if time.time() < t2 - 2.0:
        h2, c2 = newton_active_refine(h, n, t2)
        if c2 < bestc:
            bestc = c2
            besth = h2.copy()
            h = h2.copy()

    t3 = min(t_end, time.time() + max(5.0, 0.15 * alloc))
    if time.time() < t3 - 1.0:
        h3, c3 = projected_gradient_refine(h, n, t3)
        if c3 < bestc:
            bestc = c3
            besth = h3.copy()
            h = h3.copy()

    t4 = min(t_end, time.time() + max(4.0, 0.20 * alloc))
    if time.time() < t4 - 1.0:
        h4, c4 = pair_gradient_refine(h, n, t4)
        if c4 < bestc:
            bestc = c4
            besth = h4.copy()
            h = h4.copy()

    if time.time() < t_end - 2.0:
        hs, cs = try_symmetrize(h)
        if cs < bestc:
            bestc = cs
            besth = hs.copy()
            h = hs.copy()

        if time.time() < t_end - 2.0:
            hs, cs = try_shifts(h)
            if cs < bestc:
                bestc = cs
                besth = hs.copy()
                h = hs.copy()

        if time.time() < t_end - 2.0:
            hs, cs = try_regularize(h)
            if cs < bestc:
                bestc = cs
                besth = hs.copy()
                h = hs.copy()

    h = project_box_sum(besth, 0.5 * n)
    c = exact_c5(h)
    return h, c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget - 2.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        h0 = np.full(250, 0.5, dtype=float)

    if h0.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(h0)):
        h0 = np.full(250, 0.5, dtype=float)

    n0 = int(h0.size)
    best_h = project_box_sum(h0, 0.5 * n0)
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h)
    best_n = n0

    if deadline - time.time() < 20.0:
        return best_h, float(best_c), best_n

    max_n = 1200
    if n0 > max_n:
        cands = [n0]
    else:
        raw = [n0, evenize(600), evenize(3 * n0), evenize(1200), evenize(4 * n0)]
        cands = []
        for nr in raw:
            nn = int(nr)
            if nn < 20:
                continue
            if nn > max_n and nn != n0:
                nn = max_n if max_n % 2 == 0 else max_n - 1
            if nn not in cands:
                cands.append(nn)

        if n0 not in cands:
            cands.insert(0, n0)

        nonbase = sorted(set(cands) - {n0})
        if len(nonbase) > 2:
            selected = [nonbase[0], nonbase[-1]]
        else:
            selected = nonbase

        cands = [n0] + sorted(selected)
        cands = cands[:3]

    total = deadline - time.time()
    reserve_final = min(60.0, max(15.0, 0.05 * total))
    reserve_restart = min(90.0, max(25.0, 0.08 * total))
    opt_budget = max(0.0, total - reserve_final - reserve_restart)

    if len(cands) == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    elif len(cands) == 2:
        fracs = [0.40, 0.60]
    else:
        fracs = [0.25, 0.35, 0.40]

    for idx, ntry in enumerate(cands):
        if time.time() >= deadline - reserve_final - 5.0:
            break
        alloc = fracs[idx] * opt_budget
        if idx == 0:
            alloc = max(alloc, 20.0)
        else:
            if alloc < 25.0:
                break

        hd, cd = refine_candidate(
            best_h,
            int(ntry),
            min(deadline - reserve_final - 5.0, time.time() + alloc),
            alloc
        )
        if cd < best_c:
            best_c = cd
            best_h = hd
            best_n = int(hd.size)

    if time.time() < deadline - reserve_final - 5.0:
        amps = (0.04, 0.09, 0.16, 0.26)
        for amp in amps:
            if time.time() >= deadline - reserve_final - 5.0:
                break
            htry = fourier_perturb(best_h, rng, amp)
            alloc = min(22.0, deadline - time.time() - reserve_final - 5.0)
            if alloc < 8.0:
                break
            hd, cd = quick_refine(
                htry,
                best_n,
                min(deadline - reserve_final - 2.0, time.time() + alloc),
                alloc
            )
            if cd < best_c:
                best_c = cd
                best_h = hd
                best_n = int(hd.size)

    remaining = deadline - time.time()
    if remaining > 10.0:
        hd, cd = refine_candidate(best_h, best_n, deadline - 2.0, remaining - 2.0)
        if cd < best_c:
            best_c = cd
            best_h = hd
            best_n = int(hd.size)

    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, 0.5 * best_n)
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_h.size)
```
